+  /**
+   * THE WINDOW'S OWN SITE while the agent's browser is its panel: codeboss.localhost (Chrome resolves every *.localhost
+   * to this machine itself, never asking DNS) - another site than localhost, so the developer's apps on localhost are

+  static Inst inst(int id) { synchronized (LOCK) { return ALL.get(Integer.valueOf(id <= 0 ? 1 : id)); } }

+  /** Is the agent's browser this instance's panel (Settings > Agent's browser)? Read from the window's profile the first time. */
+  static boolean panelOn(Inst i) {
+    if (i == null) return false;
+    if (!i.panelRead) { i.panelRead = true; i.panel = new java.io.File(windowProfile(i), "cb-panel").isFile(); }
+    return i.panel;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The setting, from code_boss (POST /v1/browser/panel): remembered in the window's profile; a window that is open
+   * without the port the panel needs (or with one it no longer needs) is opened again - the developer just changed it.
+   */
+  static Map<String, Object> panelMode(int id, boolean on) {
+    Inst i = inst(id);
+    if (i == null) return answer("There is no code_boss " + id + " in this container.");
+    boolean was = panelOn(i);
+    i.panel = on;
+    java.io.File flag = new java.io.File(windowProfile(i), "cb-panel");
+    try { if (on) { Files.createDirectories(flag.getParentFile().toPath()); Files.write(flag.toPath(), new byte[0]); } else Files.deleteIfExists(flag.toPath()); }
+    catch (IOException e) { /* remembered for this manager's life only */ }
+    if (!on) PanelBroker.stop(i.id);
+    boolean reopened = false;
+    Process w = i.win;
+    if (w != null && w.isAlive() && i.winPort != on && "running".equals(i.state)) {
+      closeWindow(i);
+      openWindow(i);
+      reopened = true;
+    }
+    Map<String, Object> a = answer(null);
+    a.put("id", Integer.valueOf(i.id));
+    a.put("panel", Boolean.valueOf(on));
+    a.put("was", Boolean.valueOf(was));
+    a.put("reopened", Boolean.valueOf(reopened));
+    return a;
+  }

+package cb.sandbox;
+
+import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
+import java.io.EOFException;
+import java.io.File;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.io.InputStream;
+import java.io.OutputStream;
+import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
+import java.net.InetAddress;
+import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
+import java.net.Proxy;
+import java.net.ServerSocket;
+import java.net.Socket;
+import java.net.URL;
+import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.security.MessageDigest;
+import java.security.SecureRandom;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.Arrays;
+import java.util.Base64;
+import java.util.HashSet;
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.Set;
+import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

+/**
+ * THE AGENT'S BROWSER IN CODE_BOSS'S OWN WINDOW (the developer, 2026-11-12: Settings > Agent's browser - "off means
+ * keep using external browser"). With the setting on, a plugin that asks for a browser (POST /v1/browser/open) is given
+ * THIS instead of a Chrome of its own: an address that speaks DevTools like a whole browser, but whose one page is the
+ * iframe in the right panel of code_boss's window - so the developer watches every click the agent makes.
+ *
+ * HOW: code_boss's window is opened with a debugging port while the setting is on (App.openWindow) - a port only this
+ * manager connects to. The page in the panel is on another SITE than the window (127.0.0.1, the window is localhost),
+ * so Chrome gives the iframe a process and a DevTools target of its own (the target's id is the iframe's frame id,
+ * the same for its whole life - measured, test/_panel-oopif-spike.mjs). A plugin's connection here gets a connection
+ * of its own to that Chrome, and:
+ *   - the browser-level calls are ANSWERED HERE: Target.getTargets lists one page (the panel), createTarget navigates
+ *     the panel, attachToTarget attaches to the iframe's target, closeTarget blanks the panel;
+ *   - every call on the page's session goes to the iframe's target, as it is, except: Page.navigate keeps the page off
+ *     the window's own site (localhost -> 127.0.0.1: on the window's site the iframe would join the window's process
+ *     and its target would end); Page.handleJavaScriptDialog goes to the window's session (a dialog in the iframe is
+ *     announced there, measured); Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride sizes the iframe (Chrome refuses it on an iframe);
+ *   - NOTHING ELSE of the window is reachable: no other target, no Browser.*, Target.*, Storage.*, SystemInfo.* -
+ *     refused with a sentence. (On its own session, Chrome would list every target of the browser - measured.)
+ *   - a page that refuses to be framed (X-Frame-Options, CSP frame-ancestors) is shown anyway: this broker's own
+ *     session on the iframe takes those two headers off its documents (Fetch.fulfillRequest - continueResponse does
+ *     not take; measured, test/_panel-xfo-spike.mjs). A plugin's own Fetch.enable works beside it.
+ *
+ * WHO MAY CONNECT: it listens on 127.0.0.1 out here and is carried into the container like a plugin's Chrome
+ * (Mux.listenThere). A request with an Origin header is a web page, and is refused - Chrome's own DevTools does the
+ * same. The one thing a page may load from here is /blank (the empty page the panel starts on).
+ */
+final class PanelBroker {
+  private PanelBroker() { }
+
+  static final Object LOCK = new Object();
+  private static final Map<Integer, Broker> ALL = new LinkedHashMap<Integer, Broker>();   // by instance id
+  static final SecureRandom RND = new SecureRandom();
+  static final String GUID = "258EAFA5-E914-47DA-95CA-C5AB0DC85B11";
+  /** On the page's session these whole domains reach past the page (the browser, its other targets, every site's storage). */
+  static final Set<String> DENY = new HashSet<String>(Arrays.asList("Target", "Browser", "SystemInfo", "Tethering", "Storage", "Tracing", "BackgroundService", "Extensions", "PWA", "Cast", "DeviceAccess", "FedCm", "Autofill"));
+  /** Calls whose effect a session keeps: replayed when the page's target has to be attached again. */
+  static final Set<String> STICKY = new HashSet<String>(Arrays.asList("Page.setLifecycleEventsEnabled", "Page.addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument", "Network.setCacheDisabled", "Network.emulateNetworkConditions", "Network.setExtraHTTPHeaders", "Network.setUserAgentOverride", "Network.setBlockedURLs", "Runtime.setAsyncCallStackDepth", "Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate", "Emulation.setEmulatedMedia", "Emulation.setTimezoneOverride", "Emulation.setGeolocationOverride", "Emulation.setLocaleOverride", "Emulation.setUserAgentOverride"));
+
+  static String hex(int bytes) { byte[] b = new byte[bytes]; RND.nextBytes(b); StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder(); for (byte x : b) s.append(String.format("%02X", x & 0xff)); return s.toString(); }
+
+  static Map<String, Object> no(String why) { Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); m.put("ok", Boolean.FALSE); m.put("error", why); return m; }
+
+  /**
+   * THE WINDOW HAS A SITE OF ITS OWN (App.WINDOW_HOST, codeboss.localhost - Chrome resolves every *.localhost to this
+   * machine, and it is another site than localhost), so an app is shown at ITS OWN address: localhost stays localhost,
+   * and the frames it loads at its own address stay same-origin with it - searchable from the page, as in any Chrome
+   * (measured, test/_panel-nested-spike.mjs: rewriting the app to 127.0.0.1 made its localhost frames cross-origin).
+   * Only an address on the window's own site is moved (to 127.0.0.1): there the iframe would join the window's process.
+   */
+  static String siteOf(String u) {
+    if (u == null) return "";
+    return u.replaceFirst("^(?i)(https?://)" + App.WINDOW_HOST.replace(".", "\\.") + "(?=[:/?#]|$)", "$1127.0.0.1");
+  }
+
+  // ── the broker of one code_boss instance ──
+
+  /** The panel of this instance as a browser for a plugin: started (or reused), carried into the container. */
+  static Map<String, Object> open(App.Inst i, String url, String label) {
+    String u = url == null ? "" : url.trim();
+    if (u.isEmpty()) u = "about:blank";
+    if (u.length() > 4000 || !(u.startsWith("http://") || u.startsWith("https://") || "about:blank".equals(u))) {
+      return no("A browser is opened on an http:// or https:// address, or about:blank - not on '" + (u.length() > 80 ? u.substring(0, 80) + "..." : u) + "'.");
+    }
+    if (!"running".equals(Container.state)) return no("The container is not running, so there is nothing to connect a browser to. Start it on the container manager's page.");
+    if (i == null || !"running".equals(i.state)) return no("That code_boss is not running, so its window has no panel to open a page in.");
+    Process w = i.win;
+    if (w == null || !w.isAlive()) return no("code_boss's window is closed, and with Settings > Agent's browser on the agent's browser is the right panel of that window. Open the window (the container manager's page), or turn the setting off for a separate Chrome window.");
+    if (!i.winPort) return no("code_boss's window was opened without the port the panel needs. Turn Settings > Agent's browser off and on again (the window reopens), then ask again.");
+    Broker b;
+    boolean reused;
+    synchronized (LOCK) {
+      b = ALL.get(Integer.valueOf(i.id));
+      reused = b != null && b.alive();
+      if (!reused) {
+        if (b != null) b.stop();
+        b = new Broker(i);
+        try { b.start(); }
+        catch (IOException e) { return no("The panel's browser could not be started: " + e.getMessage()); }
+        ALL.put(Integer.valueOf(i.id), b);
+      }
+      Mux m;
+      try { m = Container.bridge(); }
+      catch (Sandbox.Failure f) { return no("The bridge into the container could not be made: " + f.getMessage()); }
+      if (b.mux != m) {
+        try { b.bridgeIn(m); }
+        catch (IOException e) { return no("The container's end of the bridge could not listen for the panel's browser: " + e.getMessage() + "."); }
+      }
+    }
+    b.label = label == null || label.trim().isEmpty() ? "default" : label.trim();
+    if (!reused || !"about:blank".equals(u)) b.tellPage(siteOf("about:blank".equals(u) ? b.blankUrl() : u), true, false);
+    Map<String, Object> r = b.brief();
+    r.put("ok", Boolean.TRUE);
+    r.put("reused", Boolean.valueOf(reused));
+    return r;
+  }
+
+  /** The panel's browser of every instance, for GET /v1/browser. */
+  static List<Object> list() {
+    List<Object> out = new ArrayList<Object>();
+    synchronized (LOCK) { for (Broker b : ALL.values()) if (b.alive()) out.add(b.brief()); }
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  /** Close by id: the panel is blanked (its broker stays, so the plugin's cached address keeps working). null: not the panel's. */
+  static Map<String, Object> close(String id) {
+    Broker found = null;
+    synchronized (LOCK) { for (Broker b : ALL.values()) if (b.id.equals(id) || ("panel".equals(id))) { found = b; break; } }
+    if (found == null) return null;
+    found.tellPage(found.blankUrl(), false, false);
+    Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+    m.put("ok", Boolean.TRUE);
+    m.put("closed", Boolean.TRUE);
+    m.put("id", found.id);
+    return m;
+  }
+
+  /** The setting went off, the instance stopped, or its window was opened again: its broker goes. */
+  static void stop(int instance) {
+    Broker b;
+    synchronized (LOCK) { b = ALL.remove(Integer.valueOf(instance)); }
+    if (b != null) b.stop();
+  }
+
+  static void closeAll() {
+    List<Broker> all;
+    synchronized (LOCK) { all = new ArrayList<Broker>(ALL.values()); ALL.clear(); }
+    for (Broker b : all) b.stop();
+  }
+
+  // ── a WebSocket, either end (RFC 6455; text messages only - DevTools sends nothing else) ──
+
+  static final class Ws {
+    final Socket s;
+    final InputStream in;
+    final OutputStream out;
+    final boolean masks;   // a client masks what it sends; a server never does
+    Ws(Socket s, boolean masks) throws IOException { this.s = s; this.in = s.getInputStream(); this.out = s.getOutputStream(); this.masks = masks; }
+
+    synchronized void send(String text) throws IOException { frame(0x1, text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)); }
+
+    private void frame(int op, byte[] p) throws IOException {
+      ByteArrayOutputStream f = new ByteArrayOutputStream(p.length + 14);
+      f.write(0x80 | op);
+      int m = masks ? 0x80 : 0;
+      if (p.length < 126) f.write(m | p.length);
+      else if (p.length < 65536) { f.write(m | 126); f.write(p.length >>> 8); f.write(p.length & 0xff); }
+      else { f.write(m | 127); for (int k = 7; k >= 0; k--) f.write(k >= 4 ? 0 : (p.length >>> (8 * k)) & 0xff); }
+      if (masks) {
+        byte[] key = new byte[4];
+        RND.nextBytes(key);
+        f.write(key, 0, 4);
+        byte[] q = new byte[p.length];
+        for (int k = 0; k < p.length; k++) q[k] = (byte) (p[k] ^ key[k & 3]);
+        f.write(q, 0, q.length);
+      } else f.write(p, 0, p.length);
+      out.write(f.toByteArray());
+      out.flush();
+    }
+
+    /** The next text message, or null when the other end closed. */
+    String read() throws IOException {
+      ByteArrayOutputStream msg = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
+      while (true) {
+        int b0 = in.read(), b1 = in.read();
+        if (b0 < 0 || b1 < 0) return null;
+        int op = b0 & 0x0f;
+        long len = b1 & 0x7f;
+        if (len == 126) len = (rd() << 8) | rd();
+        else if (len == 127) { len = 0; for (int k = 0; k < 8; k++) len = (len << 8) | rd(); }
+        if (len > 512L * 1024 * 1024) throw new IOException("a WebSocket message over 512 MB");
+        byte[] key = null;
+        if ((b1 & 0x80) != 0) { key = new byte[4]; full(key); }
+        byte[] p = new byte[(int) len];
+        full(p);
+        if (key != null) for (int k = 0; k < p.length; k++) p[k] ^= key[k & 3];
+        if (op == 0x8) { try { synchronized (this) { frame(0x8, new byte[0]); } } catch (IOException ignore) { } return null; }
+        if (op == 0x9) { synchronized (this) { frame(0xA, p); } continue; }
+        if (op == 0xA) continue;
+        msg.write(p, 0, p.length);
+        if ((b0 & 0x80) != 0) return new String(msg.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+      }
+    }
+    private long rd() throws IOException { int c = in.read(); if (c < 0) throw new EOFException(); return c; }
+    private void full(byte[] b) throws IOException { int n = 0; while (n < b.length) { int k = in.read(b, n, b.length - n); if (k < 0) throw new EOFException(); n += k; } }
+    void close() { try { s.close(); } catch (IOException ignore) { } }
+
+    /** A client connection to ws://127.0.0.1:port/path. */
+    static Ws connect(int port, String path) throws IOException {
+      Socket s = new Socket();
+      s.connect(new InetSocketAddress(InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"), port), 5000);
+      s.setTcpNoDelay(true);
+      byte[] k = new byte[16];
+      RND.nextBytes(k);
+      OutputStream o = s.getOutputStream();
+      o.write(("GET " + path + " HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" + port + "\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Key: " + Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(k) + "\r\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n\r\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+      o.flush();
+      String head = readHead(s.getInputStream());
+      if (head == null || !head.startsWith("HTTP/1.1 101")) { s.close(); throw new IOException("Chrome did not take the DevTools connection (" + (head == null ? "no answer" : head.split("\r\n")[0]) + ")"); }
+      return new Ws(s, true);
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** An HTTP head, up to the blank line (16 KB at most). null: the connection closed first. */
+  static String readHead(InputStream in) throws IOException {
+    StringBuilder h = new StringBuilder();
+    while (h.length() < 16384) {
+      int c = in.read();
+      if (c < 0) return null;
+      h.append((char) c);
+      int n = h.length();
+      if (n >= 4 && h.charAt(n - 4) == '\r' && h.charAt(n - 3) == '\n' && h.charAt(n - 2) == '\r' && h.charAt(n - 1) == '\n') return h.toString();
+    }
+    throw new IOException("a request head over 16 KB");
+  }
+
+  static final class Broker {
+    final App.Inst inst;
+    final String id;                     // the id open() answers with
+    final String targetId = hex(16);     // the panel's page, as a plugin sees it
+    final String browserPath = "/devtools/browser/" + java.util.UUID.randomUUID();
+    volatile String label = "default";
+    volatile String url = "about:blank", title = "";
+    ServerSocket ss;
+    int port, insidePort;
+    Mux mux;
+    volatile boolean stopped;
+    final List<Client> clients = new ArrayList<Client>();
+    final ExecutorService ex = Executors.newCachedThreadPool(r -> { Thread t = new Thread(r, "cb-panel-broker"); t.setDaemon(true); return t; });
+
+    Broker(App.Inst i) { this.inst = i; this.id = "panel-" + i.id; }
+
+    boolean alive() { return !stopped && ss != null && !ss.isClosed(); }
+    String blankUrl() { return "http://127.0.0.1:" + port + "/blank"; }
+    String shown(String u) { return u != null && u.startsWith(blankUrl()) ? "about:blank" : u; }
+    boolean windowsOwn(String u) { String w = "http://" + App.WINDOW_HOST + ":" + inst.port; return u != null && (u.startsWith(w + "/") || u.equals(w)); }
+
+    void start() throws IOException {
+      ss = new ServerSocket();
+      ss.bind(new InetSocketAddress(InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"), 0), 50);
+      port = ss.getLocalPort();
+      Thread t = new Thread(new Runnable() { public void run() { accept(); } }, "cb-panel-broker-accept");
+      t.setDaemon(true);
+      t.start();
+    }
+
+    void bridgeIn(Mux m) throws IOException {
+      if (mux != null && mux != m) { mux.revoke(port); if (mux.isAlive() && insidePort > 0) mux.unlistenThere(insidePort); }
+      m.permit(port);
+      try { insidePort = m.listenThere(0, port, 15000); }
+      catch (IOException e) { m.revoke(port); throw e; }
+      mux = m;
+    }
+
+    void stop() {
+      stopped = true;
+      try { if (ss != null) ss.close(); } catch (IOException ignore) { }
+      Mux m = mux;
+      mux = null;
+      if (m != null) { m.revoke(port); if (m.isAlive() && insidePort > 0) m.unlistenThere(insidePort); }
+      List<Client> cs;
+      synchronized (clients) { cs = new ArrayList<Client>(clients); clients.clear(); }
+      for (Client c : cs) c.close();
+      ex.shutdownNow();
+    }
+
+    Map<String, Object> brief() {
+      Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      m.put("id", id);
+      m.put("label", label);
+      m.put("url", shown(url));
+      m.put("pid", Integer.valueOf(App.pidOf(inst.win)));
+      m.put("cdpUrl", "http://127.0.0.1:" + insidePort);
+      m.put("wsUrl", "ws://127.0.0.1:" + insidePort + browserPath);
+      m.put("insidePort", Integer.valueOf(insidePort));
+      m.put("downloads", Browser.downloadsInside());
+      m.put("panel", Boolean.TRUE);
+      m.put("note", "This browser is the right panel of code_boss's window (Settings > Agent's browser): one page, which the developer watches. Target.createTarget navigates it, closeTarget blanks it; no other tab or window can be opened, and the window itself is not reachable.");
+      boolean bridged = mux != null && mux.isAlive();
+      m.put("bridged", Boolean.valueOf(bridged));
+      return m;
+    }
+
+    /** Say to code_boss's page what the panel shows: url (null: keep it), show (open the panel on it), at (it is there already: do not load it). */
+    void tellPage(String u, boolean show, boolean at) {
+      if (u != null && !at) url = u;
+      Map<String, Object> body = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      if (u != null) body.put("url", u);
+      body.put("show", Boolean.valueOf(show));
+      if (at) body.put("at", Boolean.TRUE);
+      HttpURLConnection c = null;
+      try {
+        c = (HttpURLConnection) new URL("http://127.0.0.1:" + inst.port + "/api/panel-browser").openConnection(Proxy.NO_PROXY);
+        c.setConnectTimeout(2000);
+        c.setReadTimeout(5000);
+        c.setRequestMethod("POST");
+        c.setDoOutput(true);
+        c.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json");
+        try (OutputStream o = c.getOutputStream()) { o.write(Json.write(body).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)); }
+        c.getResponseCode();
+      } catch (IOException ignore) {
+      } finally { if (c != null) c.disconnect(); }
+    }
+
+    /** Chrome's DevTools port of the window, from its profile (it writes DevToolsActivePort when it opens it). */
+    String[] chromeEndpoint() throws IOException {
+      // a window that was just opened (or opened again, when the setting changed) writes it within a second or two
+      File f = new File(App.windowProfile(inst), "DevToolsActivePort");
+      long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + 15000;
+      while (true) {
+        try {
+          String[] lines = new String(Files.readAllBytes(f.toPath()), StandardCharsets.UTF_8).split("\\r?\\n");
+          if (lines.length >= 2 && lines[0].trim().matches("\\d{2,5}") && lines[1].trim().startsWith("/devtools/")) return new String[] { lines[0].trim(), lines[1].trim() };
+        } catch (IOException notYet) { }
+        if (System.currentTimeMillis() > until || stopped) throw new IOException("code_boss's window has not opened its DevTools port (is the window open?)");
+        Sandbox.sleep(150);
+      }
+    }
+
+    private void accept() {
+      while (!stopped) {
+        final Socket s;
+        try { s = ss.accept(); } catch (IOException e) { if (stopped) return; continue; }
+        ex.submit(new Runnable() { public void run() { serve(s); } });
+      }
+    }
+
+    private void serve(Socket s) {
+      try {
+        s.setTcpNoDelay(true);
+        s.setSoTimeout(15000);
+        InputStream in = s.getInputStream();
+        String head = readHead(in);
+        if (head == null) { s.close(); return; }
+        String[] lines = head.split("\r\n");
+        String[] rl = lines[0].split(" ");
+        String method = rl[0], path = rl.length > 1 ? rl[1] : "/";
+        Map<String, String> h = new LinkedHashMap<String, String>();
+        for (int k = 1; k < lines.length; k++) { int c = lines[k].indexOf(':'); if (c > 0) h.put(lines[k].substring(0, c).trim().toLowerCase(), lines[k].substring(c + 1).trim()); }
+        String host = h.containsKey("host") ? h.get("host") : "127.0.0.1:" + port;
+        String hostName = host.startsWith("[") ? host.substring(0, host.indexOf(']') + 1) : host.split(":")[0];
+        boolean local = "127.0.0.1".equals(hostName) || "localhost".equalsIgnoreCase(hostName);
+        if ("GET".equals(method) && (path.equals("/blank") || path.startsWith("/blank?"))) {   // the page the panel starts on: nothing in it
+          respond(s, 200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", "<!doctype html><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><title></title><body style=\"margin:0;background:#fff\"></body>");
+          return;
+        }
+        if (!local) { respond(s, 403, "text/plain", "Host must be 127.0.0.1 or localhost."); return; }
+        if (h.containsKey("origin")) { respond(s, 403, "text/plain", "Rejected a connection from a web page (it sent an Origin). code_boss's panel browser takes DevTools clients only."); return; }
+        String p = path.split("\\?")[0];
+        if ("websocket".equalsIgnoreCase(h.get("upgrade"))) {
+          boolean page = p.equals("/devtools/page/" + targetId);
+          if (!p.equals(browserPath) && !page) { respond(s, 404, "text/plain", "No such DevTools target here: the browser is " + browserPath + ", its one page /devtools/page/" + targetId + "."); return; }
+          String key = h.get("sec-websocket-key");
+          if (key == null) { respond(s, 400, "text/plain", "no Sec-WebSocket-Key"); return; }
+          String accept = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-1").digest((key + GUID).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
+          OutputStream o = s.getOutputStream();
+          o.write(("HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Accept: " + accept + "\r\n\r\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+          o.flush();
+          s.setSoTimeout(0);
+          Client c = new Client(this, new Ws(s, false), page);
+          synchronized (clients) { clients.add(c); }
+          try { c.run(); } finally { synchronized (clients) { clients.remove(c); } }
+          return;
+        }
+        if (p.equals("/json/version")) {
+          Map<String, Object> v = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+          String browser = "Chrome", ua = "";
+          try { Map<String, Object> real = Json.object(Json.parse(httpGet(Integer.parseInt(chromeEndpoint()[0]), "/json/version"))); browser = Json.string(real.get("Browser")); ua = Json.string(real.get("User-Agent")); } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+          v.put("Browser", browser);
+          v.put("Protocol-Version", "1.3");
+          v.put("User-Agent", ua);
+          v.put("code_boss", "the right panel of code_boss's window (Settings > Agent's browser)");
+          v.put("webSocketDebuggerUrl", "ws://" + host + browserPath);
+          respond(s, 200, "application/json", Json.write(v));
+          return;
+        }
+        if (p.equals("/json") || p.equals("/json/list")) { List<Object> l = new ArrayList<Object>(); l.add(listed(host)); respond(s, 200, "application/json", Json.write(l)); return; }
+        if (p.equals("/json/new")) {
+          String q = path.contains("?") ? java.net.URLDecoder.decode(path.substring(path.indexOf('?') + 1), "UTF-8") : "about:blank";
+          tellPage(siteOf(q.isEmpty() || "about:blank".equals(q) ? blankUrl() : q), true, false);
+          respond(s, 200, "application/json", Json.write(listed(host)));
+          return;
+        }
+        if (p.equals("/json/activate/" + targetId)) { tellPage(null, true, false); respond(s, 200, "text/plain", "Target activated"); return; }
+        if (p.equals("/json/close/" + targetId)) { tellPage(blankUrl(), false, false); respond(s, 200, "text/plain", "Target is closing"); return; }
+        if (p.equals("/json/protocol")) { respond(s, 200, "application/json", httpGet(Integer.parseInt(chromeEndpoint()[0]), "/json/protocol")); return; }
+        respond(s, 404, "text/plain", "Not here: /json/version, /json/list, /json/new, and the DevTools WebSocket.");
+      } catch (Exception e) {
+        try { s.close(); } catch (IOException ignore) { }
+      }
+    }
+
+    Map<String, Object> listed(String host) {
+      Map<String, Object> t = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      t.put("description", "");
+      t.put("devtoolsFrontendUrl", "");
+      t.put("id", targetId);
+      t.put("title", title);
+      t.put("type", "page");
+      t.put("url", shown(url));
+      t.put("webSocketDebuggerUrl", "ws://" + host + "/devtools/page/" + targetId);
+      return t;
+    }
+
+    Map<String, Object> info(boolean attached) {
+      Map<String, Object> t = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      t.put("targetId", targetId);
+      t.put("type", "page");
+      t.put("title", title);
+      t.put("url", shown(url));
+      t.put("attached", Boolean.valueOf(attached));
+      t.put("canAccessOpener", Boolean.FALSE);
+      t.put("browserContextId", "CB-PANEL");
+      return t;
+    }
+  }
+
+  static void respond(Socket s, int code, String type, String body) throws IOException {
+    byte[] b = body.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+    OutputStream o = s.getOutputStream();
+    o.write(("HTTP/1.1 " + code + (code == 200 ? " OK" : code == 404 ? " Not Found" : code == 403 ? " Forbidden" : " Bad Request") + "\r\nContent-Type: " + type + "\r\nContent-Length: " + b.length + "\r\nCache-Control: no-store\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+    o.write(b);
+    o.flush();
+    s.close();
+  }
+
+  static String httpGet(int port, String path) throws IOException {
+    HttpURLConnection c = (HttpURLConnection) new URL("http://127.0.0.1:" + port + path).openConnection(Proxy.NO_PROXY);
+    c.setConnectTimeout(2000);
+    c.setReadTimeout(5000);
+    try (InputStream in = c.getInputStream()) {
+      ByteArrayOutputStream b = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
+      byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
+      for (int n; (n = in.read(buf)) > 0; ) b.write(buf, 0, n);
+      return new String(b.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+    } finally { c.disconnect(); }
+  }
+
+  // ── one plugin's connection: a connection of its own to the window's Chrome, and the panel's page as its one target ──
+
+  interface Done { void with(Map<String, Object> answer); }
+
+  static final class Client {
+    final Broker br;
+    final Ws ws;            // the plugin's
+    final boolean pageMode; // connected to the page's own address: every message is the page's, with no sessionId
+    Ws up;                  // to Chrome
+    final AtomicInteger seq = new AtomicInteger(1);
+    final ConcurrentHashMap<Integer, Done> pending = new ConcurrentHashMap<Integer, Done>();
+    final String sid = hex(16);   // the page's session, as the plugin knows it - the same however often the iframe is attached again
+    volatile String topSid, topFrameId, adminSid, realSid, frameId;
+    volatile boolean attached, closed;
+    // BROWSER-LEVEL AUTO-ATTACH (puppeteer finds its pages this way: Target.setAutoAttach on the browser, then the
+    // attachedToTarget it is sent for each page): on, the panel's page is attached and announced - once per attach
+    volatile boolean autoAttach, announced;
+    final List<Object[]> sticky = new ArrayList<Object[]>();
+    final Object attachLock = new Object();
+    // FRAMES INSIDE THE PAGE that are on another site (an app that loads another app): each is a target of its own in
+    // any Chrome. A plugin reaches them as it would anywhere - Target.setAutoAttach on the page's session - and their
+    // sessions are passed through as they are. Only targets INSIDE the panel's page ever get here.
+    final Set<String> children = java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(new ConcurrentHashMap<String, Boolean>());
+
+    Client(Broker br, Ws ws, boolean pageMode) { this.br = br; this.ws = ws; this.pageMode = pageMode; }
+
+    void close() { closed = true; ws.close(); Ws u = up; if (u != null) u.close(); for (Done d : pending.values()) d.with(gone()); pending.clear(); }
+    static Map<String, Object> gone() { Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); m.put("error", err(-32000, "code_boss's window closed the connection")); return m; }
+    static Map<String, Object> err(int code, String msg) { Map<String, Object> e = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); e.put("code", Integer.valueOf(code)); e.put("message", msg); return e; }
+
+    void run() {
+      try {
+        String[] ep = br.chromeEndpoint();
+        up = Ws.connect(Integer.parseInt(ep[0]), ep[1]);
+        Thread t = new Thread(new Runnable() { public void run() { upstream(); } }, "cb-panel-broker-up");
+        t.setDaemon(true);
+        t.start();
+        call("Target.setDiscoverTargets", obj("discover", Boolean.TRUE), null, null);   // the page's url and title as they change (targetInfoChanged)
+        Map<String, Object> dl = obj("behavior", "allow");
+        dl.put("downloadPath", Browser.downloadsOutside().toString());
+        call("Browser.setDownloadBehavior", dl, null, null);   // its downloads land where a plugin inside reads them, as with a Chrome of its own
+        while (!closed) {
+          String text = ws.read();
+          if (text == null) break;
+          Object parsed;
+          try { parsed = Json.parse(text); } catch (RuntimeException e) { continue; }
+          final Map<String, Object> m = Json.object(parsed);
+          // a call that waits (a page to load, the iframe to be attached) does not hold up the next
+          br.ex.submit(new Runnable() { public void run() { try { handle(m); } catch (Exception e) { reply(m.get("id"), null, err(-32000, "code_boss's panel browser: " + e.getMessage()), m.containsKey("sessionId")); } } });
+        }
+      } catch (IOException e) {
+        // Chrome's port is gone (the window closed or reopened): the plugin's connection ends with it
+      } finally { close(); }
+    }
+
+    // ── to and from Chrome ──
+
+    void call(String method, Map<String, Object> params, String session, Done done) {
+      int id = seq.getAndIncrement();
+      Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      m.put("id", Integer.valueOf(id));
+      m.put("method", method);
+      m.put("params", params == null ? new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>() : params);
+      if (session != null) m.put("sessionId", session);
+      if (done != null) pending.put(Integer.valueOf(id), done);
+      try { up.send(Json.write(m)); } catch (IOException e) { Done d = pending.remove(Integer.valueOf(id)); if (d != null) d.with(gone()); }
+    }
+
+    Map<String, Object> callSync(String method, Map<String, Object> params, String session, long ms) {
+      final Object[] box = new Object[1];
+      final CountDownLatch l = new CountDownLatch(1);
+      call(method, params, session, new Done() { public void with(Map<String, Object> a) { box[0] = a; l.countDown(); } });
+      try { if (!l.await(ms, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) return Json.object(obj("error", err(-32000, method + " had no answer in " + (ms / 1000) + " s"))); }
+      catch (InterruptedException e) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); }
+      return Json.object(box[0]);
+    }
+
+    static Map<String, Object> obj(String k, Object v) { Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); m.put(k, v); return m; }
+    static Map<String, Object> result(Map<String, Object> a) { return Json.object(a.get("result")); }
+    static String errOf(Map<String, Object> a) { Object e = a.get("error"); return e == null ? null : Json.string(Json.object(e).get("message")); }
+
+    private void upstream() {
+      try {
+        while (!closed) {
+          String text = up.read();
+          if (text == null) break;
+          Map<String, Object> m;
+          try { m = Json.object(Json.parse(text)); } catch (RuntimeException e) { continue; }
+          Object id = m.get("id");
+          if (id != null) { Done d = pending.remove(Integer.valueOf(Json.integer(id))); if (d != null) d.with(m); continue; }
+          String method = Json.string(m.get("method"));
+          String s = m.containsKey("sessionId") ? Json.string(m.get("sessionId")) : "";
+          Map<String, Object> p = Json.object(m.get("params"));
+          if (!s.isEmpty() && (s.equals(realSid) || children.contains(s))) {   // the page's own events (and its inner frames'), as they are
+            if ("Target.attachedToTarget".equals(method)) children.add(Json.string(p.get("sessionId")));
+            if ("Target.detachedFromTarget".equals(method)) children.remove(Json.string(p.get("sessionId")));
+            if (s.equals(realSid)) { topFrame(method, p); event(method, p); }
+            else passThrough(m);
+            continue;
+          }
+          if (s.isEmpty()) {
+            if ("Target.detachedFromTarget".equals(method)) {
+              String gone = Json.string(p.get("sessionId"));
+              if (gone.equals(realSid)) { realSid = null; attached = false; br.ex.submit(new Runnable() { public void run() { reattach(); } }); }
+              if (gone.equals(adminSid)) adminSid = null;
+              if (gone.equals(topSid)) topSid = null;
+            } else if ("Target.targetInfoChanged".equals(method)) {
+              Map<String, Object> ti = Json.object(p.get("targetInfo"));
+              if (frameId != null && frameId.equals(Json.string(ti.get("targetId")))) { br.url = Json.string(ti.get("url")); br.title = Json.string(ti.get("title")); }
+            }
+            continue;
+          }
+          if ("Fetch.requestPaused".equals(method) && (s.equals(adminSid) || s.equals(topSid))) { final String fs = s; final Map<String, Object> fp = p; br.ex.submit(new Runnable() { public void run() { framable(fs, fp); } }); continue; }
+          if ("Page.javascriptDialogOpening".equals(method) && s.equals(topSid) && !br.windowsOwn(Json.string(p.get("url"))) && attached) { event(method, p); continue; }   // a dialog in the iframe is announced on the window's session
+          if ("Page.javascriptDialogClosed".equals(method) && s.equals(topSid) && attached) { event(method, p); continue; }
+          if ("Page.frameNavigated".equals(method) && s.equals(adminSid)) {
+            Map<String, Object> f = Json.object(p.get("frame"));
+            if (frameId != null && frameId.equals(Json.string(f.get("id")))) { String u = Json.string(f.get("url")); br.url = u; br.tellPage(u, false, true); }
+          }
+        }
+      } catch (IOException e) { /* Chrome closed it */ }
+      finally { close(); }
+    }
+
+    /** A document that refuses to be framed is answered without the two headers that refuse it; anything else goes on as it is. */
+    private void framable(String s, Map<String, Object> p) {
+      String rid = Json.string(p.get("requestId"));
+      List<Object> hs = p.containsKey("responseHeaders") ? Json.array(p.get("responseHeaders")) : null;
+      boolean own = s.equals(topSid) && topFrameId != null && topFrameId.equals(Json.string(p.get("frameId")));
+      boolean refuses = false;
+      List<Object> kept = new ArrayList<Object>();
+      if (hs != null && !own) {
+        for (Object o : hs) {
+          Map<String, Object> hh = Json.object(o);
+          String n = Json.string(hh.get("name")).toLowerCase();
+          if ("x-frame-options".equals(n)) { refuses = true; continue; }
+          if ("content-security-policy".equals(n) && Json.string(hh.get("value")).toLowerCase().contains("frame-ancestors")) {
+            refuses = true;
+            StringBuilder v = new StringBuilder();
+            for (String d : Json.string(hh.get("value")).split(";")) if (!d.trim().toLowerCase().startsWith("frame-ancestors")) { if (v.length() > 0) v.append(';'); v.append(d); }
+            if (v.toString().trim().isEmpty()) continue;
+            Map<String, Object> nh = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); nh.put("name", hh.get("name")); nh.put("value", v.toString()); kept.add(nh);
+            continue;
+          }
+          kept.add(hh);
+        }
+      }
+      if (refuses) {
+        Map<String, Object> b = callSync("Fetch.getResponseBody", obj("requestId", rid), s, 20000);
+        if (errOf(b) == null) {
+          Map<String, Object> r = result(b);
+          String body = Json.string(r.get("body"));
+          if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(r.get("base64Encoded"))) body = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(body.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+          Map<String, Object> f = obj("requestId", rid);
+          f.put("responseCode", Integer.valueOf(Json.integer(p.get("responseStatusCode"))));
+          f.put("responseHeaders", kept);
+          f.put("body", body);
+          Map<String, Object> a = callSync("Fetch.fulfillRequest", f, s, 20000);
+          if (errOf(a) == null) return;
+        }
+      }
+      call("Fetch.continueResponse", obj("requestId", rid), s, null);
+    }
+
+    // ── to the plugin ──
+
+    void send(Map<String, Object> m) { try { ws.send(Json.write(m)); } catch (IOException e) { close(); } }
+
+    void reply(Object id, Map<String, Object> res, Map<String, Object> error, boolean onSession) {
+      Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      m.put("id", id);
+      if (error != null) m.put("error", error); else m.put("result", res == null ? new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>() : res);
+      if (onSession && !pageMode) m.put("sessionId", sid);
+      send(m);
+    }
+
+    void event(String method, Map<String, Object> params) {
+      Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      m.put("method", method);
+      m.put("params", params);
+      if (!pageMode) m.put("sessionId", sid);
+      send(m);
+    }
+
+    void browserEvent(String method, Map<String, Object> params) {
+      if (pageMode) return;
+      Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      m.put("method", method);
+      m.put("params", params);
+      send(m);
+    }
+
+    void handle(Map<String, Object> m) {
+      Object id = m.get("id");
+      String method = Json.string(m.get("method"));
+      Map<String, Object> p = Json.object(m.get("params"));
+      boolean hasSession = m.containsKey("sessionId");
+      String s = hasSession ? Json.string(m.get("sessionId")) : (pageMode ? sid : "");
+      if (s.isEmpty()) { browserLevel(id, method, p); return; }
+      if (hasSession && children.contains(s)) { innerLevel(id, method, p, s); return; }
+      if (!s.equals(sid)) { reply(id, null, err(-32001, "Session with given id not found."), hasSession); return; }
+      pageLevel(id, method, p, hasSession);
+    }
+
+    private void browserLevel(Object id, String method, Map<String, Object> p) {
+      String t = Json.string(p.get("targetId"));
+      boolean ours = t.isEmpty() || t.equals(br.targetId);
+      if ("Target.getTargets".equals(method)) { List<Object> l = new ArrayList<Object>(); l.add(br.info(attached)); reply(id, obj("targetInfos", l), null, false); return; }
+      if ("Target.getTargetInfo".equals(method)) { if (!ours) { reply(id, null, err(-32602, "No target with given id found"), false); return; } reply(id, obj("targetInfo", br.info(attached)), null, false); return; }
+      if ("Target.setDiscoverTargets".equals(method)) { if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(p.get("discover"))) browserEvent("Target.targetCreated", obj("targetInfo", br.info(attached))); reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+      if ("Target.setAutoAttach".equals(method)) {
+        autoAttach = Boolean.TRUE.equals(p.get("autoAttach"));
+        if (autoAttach) announce();
+        reply(id, null, null, false);
+        return;
+      }
+      if ("Target.setRemoteLocations".equals(method)) { reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+      if ("Target.getBrowserContexts".equals(method)) { reply(id, obj("browserContextIds", new ArrayList<Object>()), null, false); return; }
+      if ("Target.createTarget".equals(method)) {
+        String u = Json.string(p.get("url")).trim();
+        navigate(u.isEmpty() ? "about:blank" : u);
+        if (autoAttach) announce();   // (a client that auto-attaches waits for the page's attachedToTarget)
+        reply(id, obj("targetId", br.targetId), null, false);
+        return;
+      }
+      if ("Target.attachToTarget".equals(method)) {
+        if (!ours) { reply(id, null, err(-32602, "No target with given id found (code_boss's panel browser has one page: " + br.targetId + ")"), false); return; }
+        String why = attach();
+        if (why != null) { reply(id, null, err(-32000, why), false); return; }
+        Map<String, Object> ev = obj("sessionId", sid);
+        ev.put("targetInfo", br.info(true));
+        ev.put("waitingForDebugger", Boolean.FALSE);
+        browserEvent("Target.attachedToTarget", ev);
+        announced = true;
+        reply(id, obj("sessionId", sid), null, false);
+        return;
+      }
+      if ("Target.detachFromTarget".equals(method)) { detach(); Map<String, Object> ev = obj("sessionId", sid); ev.put("targetId", br.targetId); browserEvent("Target.detachedFromTarget", ev); reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+      if ("Target.closeTarget".equals(method)) { if (!ours) { reply(id, null, err(-32602, "No target with given id found"), false); return; } closePage(); reply(id, obj("success", Boolean.TRUE), null, false); return; }
+      if ("Target.activateTarget".equals(method)) { br.tellPage(null, true, false); reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+      if ("Browser.getVersion".equals(method)) { call(method, p, null, relay(id, false)); return; }
+      reply(id, null, err(-32601, "'" + method + "' is not available in code_boss's panel browser: it has one page (the right panel of code_boss's window). Use Target.getTargets / createTarget / attachToTarget / closeTarget, then everything else on that page's session."), false);
+    }
+
+    private void pageLevel(Object id, String method, Map<String, Object> p, boolean onSession) {
+      int dot = method.indexOf('.');
+      String domain = dot > 0 ? method.substring(0, dot) : method;
+      if ("Target.setDiscoverTargets".equals(method)) { reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+      if ("Target.setAutoAttach".equals(method)) {   // the frames inside the page that are targets of their own
+        if (!attached) { String why = attach(); if (why != null) { reply(id, null, err(-32000, why), onSession); return; } }
+        p.put("flatten", Boolean.TRUE);
+        remember(method, p);
+        call(method, p, realSid, relay(id, onSession));
+        return;
+      }
+      if (DENY.contains(domain)) { reply(id, null, err(-32601, "'" + method + "' is not available on code_boss's panel page: it reaches past the page (the browser, its other tabs, or other sites' storage)."), onSession); return; }
+      if ("Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride".equals(method)) { size(Json.integer(p.get("width")), Json.integer(p.get("height"))); reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+      if ("Emulation.clearDeviceMetricsOverride".equals(method)) { size(0, 0); reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+      if ("Page.bringToFront".equals(method)) { br.tellPage(null, true, false); reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+      if ("Page.close".equals(method)) { closePage(); reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+      if (!attached) { String why = attach(); if (why != null) { reply(id, null, err(-32000, why), onSession); return; } }
+      if ("Page.handleJavaScriptDialog".equals(method)) { call(method, p, topSid, relay(id, onSession)); return; }   // the dialog is the window's to answer (measured)
+      if ("Page.navigate".equals(method)) {
+        String u = Json.string(p.get("url"));
+        p.put("url", siteOf(u));
+        if (p.containsKey("frameId") && frameId != null && Json.string(p.get("frameId")).equals(br.targetId)) p.put("frameId", frameId);
+      }
+      remember(method, p);
+      String rs = realSid;
+      if (rs == null) { reply(id, null, err(-32000, "code_boss's panel page is being attached again (its page changed process); ask again"), onSession); return; }
+      call(method, p, rs, "Page.getFrameTree".equals(method) || "Page.getResourceTree".equals(method) ? topTree(id, onSession) : relay(id, onSession));
+    }
+
+    /** A frame inside the page, on its own session: the same rules as the page's, and nothing of the window. */
+    private void innerLevel(Object id, String method, Map<String, Object> p, String s) {
+      int dot = method.indexOf('.');
+      String domain = dot > 0 ? method.substring(0, dot) : method;
+      if ("Target.setAutoAttach".equals(method)) p.put("flatten", Boolean.TRUE);
+      else if (DENY.contains(domain)) { Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); m.put("id", id); m.put("error", err(-32601, "'" + method + "' is not available on a frame in code_boss's panel page.")); m.put("sessionId", s); send(m); return; }
+      if ("Page.handleJavaScriptDialog".equals(method)) { call(method, p, topSid, relayAs(id, s)); return; }
+      if ("Page.navigate".equals(method)) p.put("url", siteOf(Json.string(p.get("url"))));
+      call(method, p, s, relayAs(id, s));
+    }
+
+    private Done relayAs(final Object id, final String s) {
+      return new Done() { public void with(Map<String, Object> a) {
+        Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+        m.put("id", id);
+        if (a.containsKey("error")) m.put("error", a.get("error")); else m.put("result", a.containsKey("result") ? a.get("result") : new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>());
+        m.put("sessionId", s);
+        send(m);
+      } };
+    }
+
+    private void passThrough(Map<String, Object> m) { send(m); }
+
+    /**
+     * THE PANEL'S FRAME IS THE PAGE'S TOP FRAME, as a plugin sees it: Chrome gives an iframe's own target a parentId (the
+     * frame in code_boss's window it sits in - measured), and a plugin takes "no parentId" to mean the top frame (the
+     * prod plugin re-reads the page's address and re-installs its runtime on it). So it is left out.
+     */
+    private void topFrame(String method, Map<String, Object> p) {
+      if (!"Page.frameNavigated".equals(method) && !"Page.frameAttached".equals(method)) return;
+      Map<String, Object> f = Json.object(p.get("frame"));
+      if (frameId != null && frameId.equals(Json.string(f.get("id")))) f.remove("parentId");
+      if ("Page.frameAttached".equals(method) && frameId != null && frameId.equals(Json.string(p.get("frameId")))) p.remove("parentFrameId");
+    }
+
+    private Done topTree(final Object id, final boolean onSession) {
+      final Done r = relay(id, onSession);
+      return new Done() { public void with(Map<String, Object> a) {
+        Map<String, Object> res = Json.object(a.get("result"));
+        Map<String, Object> tree = Json.object(res.containsKey("frameTree") ? res.get("frameTree") : res.get("frameTree"));
+        Json.object(tree.get("frame")).remove("parentId");
+        r.with(a);
+      } };
+    }
+
+    private Done relay(final Object id, final boolean onSession) {
+      return new Done() { public void with(Map<String, Object> a) {
+        Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+        m.put("id", id);
+        if (a.containsKey("error")) m.put("error", a.get("error")); else m.put("result", a.containsKey("result") ? a.get("result") : new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>());
+        if (onSession && !pageMode) m.put("sessionId", sid);
+        send(m);
+      } };
+    }
+
+    private void remember(String method, Map<String, Object> p) {
+      boolean enable = method.endsWith(".enable"), disable = method.endsWith(".disable");
+      if (!enable && !disable && !STICKY.contains(method)) return;
+      synchronized (sticky) {
+        String domain = method.substring(0, method.indexOf('.'));
+        if (disable) { for (int k = sticky.size() - 1; k >= 0; k--) if (((String) sticky.get(k)[0]).equals(domain + ".enable")) sticky.remove(k); return; }
+        if (!"Page.addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument".equals(method)) for (int k = sticky.size() - 1; k >= 0; k--) if (((String) sticky.get(k)[0]).equals(method)) sticky.remove(k);
+        sticky.add(new Object[] { method, new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(p) });
+      }
+    }
+
+    // ── the panel's iframe ──
+
+    /** The iframe's frame id, read from code_boss's page (it is also the id of the iframe's own target, when it has one). */
+    private String findFrame() {
+      if (topSid == null) {
+        List<Object> ts = Json.array(result(callSync("Target.getTargets", null, null, 5000)).get("targetInfos"));
+        String top = null;
+        for (Object o : ts) { Map<String, Object> ti = Json.object(o); if ("page".equals(ti.get("type")) && br.windowsOwn(Json.string(ti.get("url")))) { top = Json.string(ti.get("targetId")); break; } }
+        if (top == null) return null;
+        Map<String, Object> a = callSync("Target.attachToTarget", attachParams(top), null, 5000);
+        if (errOf(a) != null) return null;
+        topSid = Json.string(result(a).get("sessionId"));
+        topFrameId = top;
+        callSync("Page.enable", null, topSid, 5000);   // dialogs in the iframe are announced on the window's session
+        callSync("Fetch.enable", fetchPatterns(), topSid, 5000);   // the iframe's first document (before it has a target of its own)
+      }
+      Map<String, Object> e = callSync("Runtime.evaluate", obj("expression", "document.getElementById('agentFrame')"), topSid, 5000);
+      String oid = Json.string(Json.object(result(e).get("result")).get("objectId"));
+      if (oid.isEmpty()) return null;
+      Map<String, Object> d = callSync("DOM.describeNode", obj("objectId", oid), topSid, 5000);
+      String f = Json.string(Json.object(result(d).get("node")).get("frameId"));
+      callSync("Runtime.releaseObject", obj("objectId", oid), topSid, 2000);
+      return f.isEmpty() ? null : f;
+    }
+
+    static Map<String, Object> attachParams(String targetId) { Map<String, Object> m = obj("targetId", targetId); m.put("flatten", Boolean.TRUE); return m; }
+    static Map<String, Object> fetchPatterns() {
+      Map<String, Object> pat = obj("urlPattern", "*");
+      pat.put("resourceType", "Document");
+      pat.put("requestStage", "Response");
+      List<Object> l = new ArrayList<Object>();
+      l.add(pat);
+      return obj("patterns", l);
+    }
+
+    /** Attach the plugin's session to the iframe's own target (giving the iframe one first, if it is still blank). null, or why not. */
+    String attach() {
+      synchronized (attachLock) {
+        if (attached && realSid != null) return null;
+        long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + 20000;
+        boolean asked = false;
+        while (!closed && System.currentTimeMillis() < until) {
+          String f = findFrame();
+          if (f == null) {
+            if (topSid == null) return "code_boss's window was not found among the pages of its Chrome. Is it open?";
+            return "code_boss's window has no panel browser in it. Is Settings > Agent's browser on?";
+          }
+          frameId = f;
+          Map<String, Object> a = callSync("Target.attachToTarget", attachParams(f), null, 5000);
+          if (errOf(a) != null) {
+            // no target of its own yet: the iframe is still on about:blank, on the window's site. Put it on the blank page out here.
+            if (!asked) { br.tellPage(br.blankUrl(), false, false); asked = true; }
+            Sandbox.sleep(250);
+            continue;
+          }
+          // the first session is this broker's own (address line, framing); the plugin's is the next. Once the broker has
+          // its own, the probe above already is the plugin's.
+          String probe = Json.string(result(a).get("sessionId"));
+          if (adminSid == null) {
+            adminSid = probe;
+            callSync("Page.enable", null, probe, 5000);
+            callSync("Fetch.enable", fetchPatterns(), probe, 5000);
+            Map<String, Object> b = callSync("Target.attachToTarget", attachParams(f), null, 5000);
+            if (errOf(b) != null) return "code_boss's panel page could not be attached: " + errOf(b);
+            probe = Json.string(result(b).get("sessionId"));
+          }
+          realSid = probe;
+          attached = true;
+          List<Object[]> again;
+          synchronized (sticky) { again = new ArrayList<Object[]>(sticky); }
+          for (Object[] c : again) callSync((String) c[0], Json.object(c[1]), realSid, 5000);
+          return null;
+        }
+        return "code_boss's panel did not open a page of its own within 20 s.";
+      }
+    }
+
+    /** The iframe went to the window's own site (its target ended): put it back on 127.0.0.1, attach again, replay the session's settings. */
+    private void reattach() {
+      if (closed) return;
+      String u = siteOf(br.url);
+      br.tellPage(u.isEmpty() || "about:blank".equals(u) ? br.blankUrl() : u, false, false);
+      Sandbox.sleep(300);
+      attach();
+    }
+
+    /** The page attached, and said so to a client that auto-attaches - once per attach. */
+    private void announce() {
+      if (announced) return;
+      if (attach() != null) return;
+      announced = true;
+      Map<String, Object> ev = obj("sessionId", sid);
+      ev.put("targetInfo", br.info(true));
+      ev.put("waitingForDebugger", Boolean.FALSE);
+      browserEvent("Target.attachedToTarget", ev);
+    }
+
+    private void detach() {
+      announced = false;
+      String r = realSid;
+      realSid = null;
+      attached = false;
+      synchronized (sticky) { sticky.clear(); }
+      if (r != null) call("Target.detachFromTarget", obj("sessionId", r), null, null);
+    }
+
+    private void closePage() {
+      detach();
+      Map<String, Object> ev = obj("sessionId", sid);
+      ev.put("targetId", br.targetId);
+      browserEvent("Target.detachedFromTarget", ev);
+      br.tellPage(br.blankUrl(), false, false);
+    }
+
+    /** Target.createTarget: the panel goes to the address, and the call returns once the iframe has left the page it was on. */
+    private void navigate(String u) {
+      String to = "about:blank".equals(u) ? br.blankUrl() : siteOf(u);
+      String before = br.url;
+      br.tellPage(to, true, false);
+      long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + 20000;
+      while (!closed && System.currentTimeMillis() < until) {
+        if (frameId == null) frameId = findFrame();
+        if (frameId != null) {
+          List<Object> ts = Json.array(result(callSync("Target.getTargets", null, null, 5000)).get("targetInfos"));
+          for (Object o : ts) {
+            Map<String, Object> ti = Json.object(o);
+            if (!frameId.equals(Json.string(ti.get("targetId")))) continue;
+            String now = Json.string(ti.get("url"));
+            if (now.equals(to) || (!now.isEmpty() && !"about:blank".equals(now) && !now.equals(before))) { br.url = now; br.title = Json.string(ti.get("title")); return; }
+          }
+        }
+        Sandbox.sleep(200);
+      }
+    }
+
+    /** The page's size as the plugin asked for it: the iframe is laid out at that size and scaled to fit the panel. 0,0: the panel's own. */
+    private void size(int w, int h) {
+      if (topSid == null) findFrame();
+      if (topSid == null) return;
+      call("Runtime.evaluate", obj("expression", "window.panelBrowserSize && panelBrowserSize(" + Math.max(0, w) + "," + Math.max(0, h) + ")"), topSid, null);
+    }
+  }
+}

+/** Changed lines that differ only in whitespace - line endings, indentation, spaces at the end: a removed line and an
+ * added one, equal once every space, tab and CR is taken out. A parsed file ({ hunks }) -> how many such pairs. */
+export function gcWhitespaceOnlyLines(file) {
+  const body = (file && file.hunks ? file.hunks : []).flatMap((h) => h.body || []);
+  const squash = (l) => l.slice(1).replace(/\s+/g, '');
+  const removed = new Map();
+  for (const l of body) if (l[0] === '-' && squash(l)) { const k = squash(l); removed.set(k, (removed.get(k) || 0) + 1); }
+  let n = 0;
+  for (const l of body) { if (l[0] !== '+') continue; const k = squash(l); const c = k && removed.get(k); if (c) { removed.set(k, c - 1); n++; } }
+  return n;
+}
+
+// ── WHAT A PUSH WOULD SEND (the developer, 2026-11-12: "see the local commits and how they differ from the remote repo ...
+// as well as uncommitted changes (no need to generate a commit message), so you can see what would go if you did a
+// push"). Every git call here is one JGit (the container's git) answers: rev-parse @{u}, log <range> --format with
+// %x00/%x1e, rev-list --count, show --format= -p, diff HEAD, status --porcelain. ──
+
+/** A file of a diff as the window shows it: its path, what was added and taken out, whitespace-only lines, its hunks (capped). */
+export function gcFileView(f, { hunkLines = 400 } = {}) {
+  let adds = 0, dels = 0;
+  for (const h of f.hunks || []) for (const l of h.body || []) { if (l[0] === '+') adds++; else if (l[0] === '-') dels++; }
+  const kind = /^new file mode/m.test(f.header || '') ? 'added' : /^deleted file mode/m.test(f.header || '') ? 'deleted' : /^rename from/m.test(f.header || '') ? 'renamed' : 'modified';
+  return {
+    path: f.path, kind, adds, dels, wsOnly: gcWhitespaceOnlyLines(f), eolOnly: gcEolOnlyLines(f),
+    hunks: (f.hunks || []).map((h) => ({ header: h.header || '', lines: (h.body || []).slice(0, hunkLines), more: Math.max(0, (h.body || []).length - hunkLines) })),
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * { branch, upstream, noRemote, ahead, behind, commits: [{ sha, short, subject, author, at }], more, uncommitted: { files, untracked } }
+ * upstream: the branch's own (@{u}), else origin/<branch> when that exists; none: the branch is not on a remote - its
+ * last `max` commits are listed, and noRemote says so.
+ */
+export async function collectOutgoingAsync(repoDir, { max = 100 } = {}) {
+  const br = await gcGitAsync(repoDir, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD']);
+  const branch = br.status === 0 ? String(br.stdout || '').trim() : '';
+  let upstream = null;
+  const up = await gcGitAsync(repoDir, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', '--symbolic-full-name', '@{u}']);
+  if (up.status === 0 && String(up.stdout || '').trim() && !/@\{u\}/.test(up.stdout)) upstream = String(up.stdout).trim();
+  else if (branch && branch !== 'HEAD') { const o = await gcGitAsync(repoDir, ['rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', `refs/remotes/origin/${branch}`]); if (o.status === 0 && String(o.stdout || '').trim()) upstream = `origin/${branch}`; }
+  const range = upstream ? `${upstream}..HEAD` : 'HEAD';
+  const lg = await gcGitAsync(repoDir, ['log', `--max-count=${max + 1}`, '--format=%H%x00%h%x00%s%x00%an%x00%aI%x1e', range]);
+  const commits = lg.status === 0 ? String(lg.stdout || '').split('\x1e').map((r) => r.replace(/^\s+/, '')).filter(Boolean).map((r) => { const [sha, short, subject, author, at] = r.split('\x00'); return { sha, short, subject: subject || '', author: author || '', at: (at || '').trim() }; }).filter((c) => /^[0-9a-f]{7,40}$/.test(c.sha || '')) : [];
+  const more = commits.length > max;
+  if (more) commits.length = max;
+  let behind = 0;
+  if (upstream) { const b = await gcGitAsync(repoDir, ['rev-list', '--count', `HEAD..${upstream}`]); behind = b.status === 0 ? (parseInt(String(b.stdout).trim(), 10) || 0) : 0; }
+  const ch = await collectRepoChangesAsync(repoDir);
+  return {
+    branch, upstream, noRemote: !upstream, ahead: upstream ? commits.length : null, behind, commits, more,
+    uncommitted: { files: ch.files.map((f) => gcFileView(f)), untracked: ch.untracked },
+  };
+}
+
+/** One commit's files, each with its diff (gcFileView). null when git could not show it. */
+export async function commitFilesAsync(repoDir, sha) {
+  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{7,40}$/i.test(String(sha || ''))) return null;
+  const r = await gcGitAsync(repoDir, ['show', '--format=', '-p', sha]);
+  if (r.status !== 0) return null;
+  return parseDiffFiles(r.stdout).map((f) => gcFileView(f));
+}

+// WHAT THE WORK SAYS OF ITSELF, given with it (the developer, 2026-11-12: they were lines at the end of a reply, and the
+// reply that hands work over - commit(), done() - is the one that skips them). Three fields, all required, "none" when
+// one does not apply: shown with the work when its check passes (Checked as a check mark, a "none" left out), and handed
+// to the check as claims to test.
+export const WORK_REPORT_FIELDS = [
+  ['different', 'Different from what you asked', 'what you did other than what was asked: the asked-for method failed and you used another, steps they asked to keep apart were merged, a request that reads two ways was read one way'],
+  ['choices', 'Choices', 'what you decided that they did not spell out and a result depends on: limits in data you generated, what a word was taken to mean, a format'],
+  ['checked', 'Checked', 'how you know it is right, with what was compared - a check that CAN FAIL (not the same logic run twice); or say it is not checked'],
+];
+export function workReportOf(opt, fn) {
+  const out = {}, missing = [];
+  for (const [k] of WORK_REPORT_FIELDS) {

+THE BROWSER MAY BE CODE_BOSS'S OWN PANEL (Settings > Agent's browser, on): then open() answers with b.panel === true
+and the "browser" is the right panel of code_boss's window, where the developer watches the page. The same calls work
+(/json/version, Target.getTargets / createTarget / attachToTarget with flatten / closeTarget, then everything on the
+page's session), with these differences - write the plugin so it works either way:
+  - ONE page. createTarget navigates it (and answers the same target id); closeTarget blanks it; there are no other
+    tabs or windows. The label does not choose a profile: every label is the panel.
+  - No screenshots and no PDF (Page.captureScreenshot / printToPDF are refused on it). Read the page instead.
+  - Browser.* and Target.*/Storage.* on the page's session are refused with a sentence; the page cannot reach
+    code_boss's window. Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride sets the page's size (it is shown scaled to fit).
+  - FRAMES INSIDE THE PAGE are as in any Chrome: the page's own frame is the top frame (no parentId); frames at the
+    app's own address are same-origin and searchable from the page; a frame on another site is a target of its own,
+    reached with Target.setAutoAttach({ autoAttach: true, flatten: true }) on the page's session (that one Target.*
+    call is allowed there). Addresses are used as given - localhost stays localhost.
+  - DIALOGS (alert / confirm / prompt / beforeunload) come as Page.javascriptDialogOpening on the page's session and are
+    answered there with Page.handleJavaScriptDialog, as usual. A page that refuses to be framed (X-Frame-Options, CSP
+    frame-ancestors) is shown anyway. Downloads land in b.downloads; DOM.setFileInputFiles takes a path as usual.
+  - THE DEVELOPER SHARES THE PAGE: they may type an address in the panel or click in it. What they do comes to you as
+    it would from anyone (Page.frameNavigated with no parentId, etc.) - read the page's address again before you act on
+    what you last saw. Your own navigations are shown to them in the panel's address line.
+  - CLIENTS: a raw WebSocket (as above), puppeteer.connect({ browserWSEndpoint: b.wsUrl, defaultViewport: null }) -
+    pages(), newPage() (the panel), goto, click, type, frames() - and two clients at once all work. b.wsUrl stays the
+    same while code_boss runs, even when the developer reopens the window; a cached address is still good.
+  - THE SETTING IS THE DEVELOPER'S: a plugin cannot switch it, and should not tell them to. b.panel says which you got.

+  function enabledCheatsheet() {
+    const out = [];
+    const str = (v, n) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.trim() ? v.trim().slice(0, n) : '');
+    for (const p of enabledPlugins()) {
+      const groups = Array.isArray(p.cheatsheet) ? p.cheatsheet : [];

+        const items = [];

+        if (items.length) out.push({ header, items, plugin: p.name });
+      }
+    }
+    return out;

+    const queued = _reconcileChain;
+    let t = null;
+    return Promise.race([queued.then(() => true, () => true), new Promise((r) => { t = setTimeout(() => r(false), Math.max(0, Number(ms) || 0)); if (t.unref) t.unref(); })])

+  function rawArchiveFile(file) {

+  function rawArchivePrune(dir) {

+  function startCsTour(kind) {
+    if (!_cheat) return;
+    closeCheatSheet();
+    const entries = [...(Array.isArray(_cheat[kind]) ? _cheat[kind] : []), ...(kind === 'ui' ? csPluginTourEntries() : [])];
+    if (kind === 'settings') { try { openSettings(); } catch {} }
+    if (kind === 'git') document.querySelector('#gitProjects .gp-badge')?.click();
+    const el = document.getElementById('csTour');
+    document.getElementById('csTourName').textContent = CS_TOUR_WHAT[kind].name;
+    el.classList.add('show');
+    let raf = 0, x = -1, y = -1;
+    const draw = () => { raf = 0; if (_tour) csTourDraw(x, y); };
+    const move = (e) => { x = e.clientX; y = e.clientY; if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw); };
+    const again = () => { if (!raf && x >= 0) raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw); };
+    // (Esc ends the tour, and only the tour: not the run, not the window under it - it closes with the tour)
+    const key = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); endCsTour(true); } };
+    document.addEventListener('mousemove', move, { capture: true, passive: true });
+    window.addEventListener('scroll', again, { capture: true, passive: true });
+    window.addEventListener('resize', again);
+    window.addEventListener('keydown', key, true);
+    _tour = { kind, entries, off: () => { document.removeEventListener('mousemove', move, { capture: true }); window.removeEventListener('scroll', again, { capture: true }); window.removeEventListener('resize', again); window.removeEventListener('keydown', key, true); if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); } };
+  }

+  // anywhere by itself (not its button), kept on the screen; where it was left is where the next tour puts it
+  (function csTourBarDrag() {
+    const bar = document.getElementById('csTourBar'); if (!bar) return;
+    bar.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
+      if (e.button !== 0 || e.target.closest('button')) return;
+      e.preventDefault();

+      bar.classList.add('dragging');
+      try { bar.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch {}
+      const move = (m) => {
+        const W = window.innerWidth, H = window.innerHeight;

+  function endCsTour(back) {
+    const t = _tour; if (!t) return;

+  function csTourPick(x, y) {
+    let best = null, bestD = Infinity, bestA = Infinity;
+    const W = window.innerWidth, H = window.innerHeight;
+    for (const e of _tour.entries) {
+      let els;
+      try { els = e.el ? [e.el] : document.querySelectorAll(e.sel); } catch { continue; }
+      let k = 0;
+      for (const el of els) {
+        if (!el || !el.isConnected || el.closest('#csTour')) continue;
+        if (e.label && csLabelOf(el) !== e.label) continue;
+        const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
+        if (r.width < 2 || r.height < 2 || r.bottom < 0 || r.top > H || r.right < 0 || r.left > W) continue;
+        if (++k > 200) break;
+        const dx = Math.max(r.left - x, 0, x - r.right), dy = Math.max(r.top - y, 0, y - r.bottom);
+        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy), a = r.width * r.height;
+        // nearest; over several (a row inside the conversation), the smallest
+        if (d < bestD - 0.5 || (Math.abs(d - bestD) <= 0.5 && a < bestA)) { best = { el, r, e }; bestD = d; bestA = a; }
+      }
+    }
+    return bestD <= 160 ? best : null;
+  }
+  function csTourDraw(x, y) {
+    const box = document.getElementById('csTourBox'), tip = document.getElementById('csTourTip'), line = document.getElementById('csTourLine');
+    const c = csTourPick(x, y);
+    if (!c) { box.style.display = 'none'; tip.style.display = 'none'; line.style.display = 'none'; return; }
+    const W = window.innerWidth, H = window.innerHeight, P = 3;
+    const r = { left: c.r.left - P, top: c.r.top - P, right: c.r.right + P, bottom: c.r.bottom + P };
+    Object.assign(box.style, { display: 'block', left: r.left + 'px', top: r.top + 'px', width: (r.right - r.left) + 'px', height: (r.bottom - r.top) + 'px' });
+    if (tip._for !== c.e) {
+      tip._for = c.e; tip.textContent = '';
+      const b = document.createElement('b'); b.textContent = c.e.title || ''; tip.appendChild(b);
+      if (c.e.desc) { const s = document.createElement('span'); s.textContent = c.e.desc; tip.appendChild(s); }
+    }
+    tip.style.display = 'block'; tip.style.visibility = 'hidden'; tip.style.left = '0px'; tip.style.top = '0px';
+    const tw = tip.offsetWidth, th = tip.offsetHeight, G = 26;
+    // below, above, right, left of the part - the first with room; kept on the screen and clear of the bar at the top
+    const spots = [
+      { l: r.left, t: r.bottom + G }, { l: r.left, t: r.top - G - th },
+      { l: r.right + G, t: Math.max(r.top, 48) }, { l: r.left - G - tw, t: Math.max(r.top, 48) },
+    ];
+    const fits = (s) => s.t >= 44 && s.t + th <= H - 6 && s.l >= 6 && s.l + tw <= W - 6;
+    let s = spots.find((p) => fits({ l: Math.min(Math.max(p.l, 6), W - tw - 6), t: p.t })) || { l: x + 18, t: y + 18 };
+    s = { l: Math.min(Math.max(s.l, 6), W - tw - 6), t: Math.min(Math.max(s.t, 44), H - th - 6) };
+    Object.assign(tip.style, { left: s.l + 'px', top: s.t + 'px', visibility: 'visible' });
+    // the arrow: from the note's nearest edge to the part's
+    const cx = (r.left + r.right) / 2, cy = (r.top + r.bottom) / 2;
+    const x1 = Math.min(Math.max(cx, s.l), s.l + tw), y1 = Math.min(Math.max(cy, s.t), s.t + th);
+    const x2 = Math.min(Math.max(x1, r.left), r.right), y2 = Math.min(Math.max(y1, r.top), r.bottom);
+    if (Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1) < 10) { line.style.display = 'none'; return; }
+    line.setAttribute('x1', x1); line.setAttribute('y1', y1); line.setAttribute('x2', x2); line.setAttribute('y2', y2); line.style.display = '';
+  }

+const send = (method, params = {}, sessionId) => new Promise((r) => { const id = ++seq; waiting.set(id, r); ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params, ...(sessionId ? { sessionId } : {}) })); });
+const strip = (hs) => hs.filter((h) => !/^x-frame-options$/i.test(h.name)).map((h) => /^content-security-policy$/i.test(h.name) ? { name: h.name, value: h.value.split(';').filter((d) => !/^\s*frame-ancestors/i.test(d)).join(';') } : h);
+let paused = [];
+handlers.push(async (j) => {
+  if (j.method !== 'Fetch.requestPaused') return;
+  paused.push(`${j.sessionId === topS ? 'TOP' : 'FRAME'}:${j.params.resourceType}:${j.params.request.url.slice(-6)}`);
+  const hs = j.params.responseHeaders;
+  if (hs) { const b = await send('Fetch.getResponseBody', { requestId: j.params.requestId }, j.sessionId); const r = await send('Fetch.fulfillRequest', { requestId: j.params.requestId, responseCode: j.params.responseStatusCode, responseHeaders: strip(hs), body: b.result.base64Encoded ? b.result.body : Buffer.from(b.result.body).toString('base64') }, j.sessionId); if (r.error) console.log('   fulfill ERR', r.error.message); }
+  else await send('Fetch.continueRequest', { requestId: j.params.requestId }, j.sessionId);
+});
+let topS;
+const title = async (fs) => { const r = await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'document.title + " @ " + location.href', returnByValue: true }, fs); return r.error ? 'ERR ' + r.error.message : r.result?.result?.value; };
+const frameId = async () => { const o = (await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: "document.getElementById('agentFrame')" }, topS)).result.result.objectId; return (await send('DOM.describeNode', { objectId: o }, topS)).result.node.frameId; };
+try {
+  await sleep(800);
+  await send('Target.setDiscoverTargets', { discover: true });
+  const tp = (await send('Target.getTargets')).result.targetInfos.find((t) => t.type === 'page' && t.url.includes('localhost'));
+  topS = (await send('Target.attachToTarget', { targetId: tp.targetId, flatten: true })).result.sessionId;
+  // A: Fetch on the TOP session, the frame's first load straight onto the refusing page
+  const pat = { patterns: [{ urlPattern: '*', resourceType: 'Document', requestStage: 'Response' }] };
+  console.log('enable on top:', JSON.stringify((await send('Fetch.enable', pat, topS)).error || 'ok'));
+  await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: `document.getElementById('agentFrame').src='http://127.0.0.1:${P(xfo)}/first'` }, topS);
+  await sleep(1500);
+  const fid = await frameId();
+  const at = await send('Target.attachToTarget', { targetId: fid, flatten: true });
+  console.log('   attach:', JSON.stringify(at).slice(0, 200), (await send('Target.getTargets')).result.targetInfos.filter((t) => t.type === 'iframe').map((t) => t.targetId === fid ? 'FRAME ' + t.url : t.url));
+  let fs = at.result?.sessionId;
+  console.log('[A] top-session Fetch, first load of the refusing page:', fs ? await title(fs) : 'no frame target', paused);
+  // B: frame already on a plain page (its own target), then navigated to the refusing page: Fetch on the frame session
+  paused = [];
+  await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: `document.getElementById('agentFrame').src='http://127.0.0.1:${P(plain)}/'` }, topS);
+  await sleep(1200);
+  fs = (await send('Target.attachToTarget', { targetId: await frameId(), flatten: true })).result?.sessionId;
+  console.log('[B0] on plain:', await title(fs));
+  await send('Page.navigate', { url: `http://127.0.0.1:${P(xfo)}/second` }, fs);
+  await sleep(1500);
+  console.log('[B1] frame navigated to the refusing page, Fetch only on TOP:', await title(fs), paused);
+  paused = [];
+  await send('Page.navigate', { url: `http://127.0.0.1:${P(plain)}/` }, fs); await sleep(800);
+  console.log('enable on frame:', JSON.stringify((await send('Fetch.enable', pat, fs)).error || 'ok'));
+  await send('Page.navigate', { url: `http://127.0.0.1:${P(xfo)}/third` }, fs);
+  await sleep(1500);
+  console.log('[B2] with Fetch on the FRAME session too:', await title(fs), paused);
+  // C: a second client session on the frame with ITS OWN Fetch (the plugin's) - both pause in turn?
+  paused = [];
+  const fs2 = (await send('Target.attachToTarget', { targetId: await frameId(), flatten: true })).result?.sessionId;
+  let plug = 0; handlers.push(async (j) => { if (j.method === 'Fetch.requestPaused' && j.sessionId === fs2) { plug++; await send('Fetch.continueRequest', { requestId: j.params.requestId }, fs2); } });
+  console.log('plugin session Fetch.enable:', JSON.stringify((await send('Fetch.enable', {}, fs2)).error || 'ok'));
+  await send('Page.navigate', { url: `http://127.0.0.1:${P(xfo)}/fourth` }, fs);
+  await sleep(1500);
+  console.log('[C] two Fetch clients on the frame:', await title(fs), 'plugin saw', plug, 'requests; broker saw', paused);
+} catch (e) { console.log('spike error', e); }
+finally { ws.close(); proc.kill(); for (const s of [top, plain, xfo]) s.close(); process.exit(0); }
