=== add test/test-agents-mid-run.mjs
new 99c6c6a24dba97a9626cf18f
+/**
+ * THE AGENTS AFTER A RUN, DURING A LONG ONE TOO (the developer, 2026-11-12: "some runs are very long and the bookkeeper,
+ * heads up, etc don't run for a very long time. Let's run them at the end of the run and every 10 exchanges").
+ * Through the real server, a scripted agent that takes 25 exchanges (one snippet each, then its reply):
+ *   [1] at exchanges 10 and 20, a round: the bookkeeper's pass and Heads up, while the run is still going
+ *   [2] at the end, the usual round: the bookkeeper's pass "end of the run" and Heads up again
+ *   [3] a short run (3 exchanges): nothing mid-run, only the end
+ *   [4] the run ends while its mid-run round is still going: its own round runs when that one is done (the developer,
+ *       2026-11-12: "we don't want to sit idle w/ messages that haven't been looked at")
+ *   [5] ...unless the developer sends another message first: then that run's round is theirs
+ * Run: node test/test-agents-mid-run.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-mid-home-'));
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER = '0';
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS = '0';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-mid-proj-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'notes.txt'), 'notes\n');
+let turns = 0, plan = 25, running = false, slowMs = 0;
+const side = [];   // [{ who, atTurn, running }]
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const c = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+    const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{3,8}) ---\s*$/.exec(c) || [])[1] || 'zzzz';
+    turns++;
+    await sleep(60);   // (a turn takes a moment: the round started at a tenth has time to be seen running)
+    const text = turns < plan ? `Step ${turns}.\n<remote-workspace><js>await write("step-${turns}.txt", "step ${turns} of the checklist\n"); return "wrote step ${turns}";</js></remote-workspace>` : `All done.\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: text } }] };
+  },
+  async complete(req) {
+    const text = (req.messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n');
+    const who = /Bring the record up to date/.test(text) ? 'bookkeeper' : /heads.?up/i.test(text) ? 'heads up' : 'other';
+    side.push({ who, atTurn: turns, running, at: Date.now() });
+    if (slowMs) await sleep(slowMs);
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: who === 'bookkeeper' ? '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' : 'NOTHING' } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const PORT = 29471, B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+const post = async (p, body) => (await fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })).json().catch(() => ({}));
+const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json().catch(() => ({}));
+const logOf = async () => readFile(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'code-boss.log'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+const untilIdle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 600; i++) { const s = await state(); running = s.status === 'running'; if (!running && i > 3) return; await sleep(100); } };
+try {
+  await sleep(800);
+  await post('/api/agent-flags', { flag: 'bookkeeper', on: true });
+  console.log('\n[1] a run of 25 exchanges');
+  running = true;
+  const watch = (async () => { while (running) { running = (await state()).status === 'running'; await sleep(50); } })();
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Go through every step of the checklist, please.', model: 'm' });
+  await untilIdle(); await watch;
+  await sleep(1500);   // (the round after the run)
+  const log = await logOf();
+  const mids = [...log.matchAll(/mid-run agents: at exchange (\d+)/g)].map((m) => +m[1]);
+  ok(turns === 25, `the agent took 25 exchanges (${turns})`);
+  ok(mids.join(',') === '10,20', `a round at exchanges 10 and 20 (${mids.join(',') || 'none'})`);
+  ok(/bookkeeper: pass \(exchange 10 of the run\)/.test(log) || /bookkeeper: pass \(exchange 10 of the run\) - nothing new/.test(log) || side.some((x) => x.who === 'bookkeeper' && x.running), 'the bookkeeper\'s pass ran during the run');
+  const huMid = side.filter((x) => x.who === 'heads up' && x.atTurn < 25);
+  ok(huMid.length >= 1 && huMid[0].atTurn >= 10 && huMid[0].atTurn < 25, `Heads up checked during the run (at turn ${huMid.map((x) => x.atTurn).join(', ')})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] and at the end');
+  ok(/bookkeeper: pass \(end of the run\)/.test(log) || side.some((x) => x.who === 'bookkeeper' && !x.running), 'the bookkeeper\'s pass at the end of the run');
+  ok(side.some((x) => x.who === 'heads up' && x.atTurn === 25), 'and Heads up after it (after the 25th exchange)');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a short run');
+  turns = 0; plan = 3; side.length = 0;
+  running = true;
+  const watch2 = (async () => { while (running) { running = (await state()).status === 'running'; await sleep(50); } })();
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'One more small thing.', model: 'm' });
+  await untilIdle(); await watch2;
+  await sleep(1500);
+  const log2 = (await logOf()).slice(log.length);
+  ok(turns === 3 && !/mid-run agents/.test(log2) && side.length > 0 && side.every((x) => x.atTurn === 3), `3 exchanges: no round during it, the usual one at the end (${side.map((x) => x.who + '@' + x.atTurn).join(', ')})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] the run ends while its mid-run round is still going');
+  turns = 0; plan = 12; side.length = 0; slowMs = 2500;
+  const before4 = (await logOf()).length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Twelve steps this time.', model: 'm' });
+  await untilIdle();
+  for (let i = 0; i < 200 && !/this run's round now/.test((await logOf()).slice(before4)); i++) await sleep(100);
+  await sleep(1500);
+  const log4 = (await logOf()).slice(before4);
+  const endHu = side.filter((x) => x.who === 'heads up' && x.atTurn === 12);
+  ok(/mid-run agents: at exchange 10/.test(log4) && /a mid-run round is still going - this run's round runs when it is done/.test(log4) && /the mid-run round is done - this run's round now/.test(log4), 'the run ended during the round at exchange 10: its own round waited for it, then ran');
+  ok(endHu.length >= 1, `and Heads up looked at the end of the run (${side.map((x) => x.who + '@' + x.atTurn).join(', ')})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[5] ...unless the developer sends another message first');
+  turns = 0; plan = 12; side.length = 0; slowMs = 2500;
+  const before5 = (await logOf()).length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Twelve steps again.', model: 'm' });
+  await untilIdle();
+  slowMs = 0; plan = 15;   // (their next message: a short run)
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'And one more thing.', model: 'm' });
+  await untilIdle();
+  await sleep(4000);
+  const log5 = (await logOf()).slice(before5);
+  ok(/the developer sent another message while the mid-run round went - their run has its own round/.test(log5), 'the developer\'s next message came first: the ended run\'s round was left to theirs');
+  ok(side.some((x) => x.who === 'heads up' && x.atTurn === 15), `and their run's own round looked at everything (${side.map((x) => x.who + '@' + x.atTurn).join(', ')})`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { server.close(); }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-big-process-output.mjs
new 311e29e9bdc36bf98e65ce67
+/**
+ * A process that prints a LOT (the dev agent, 2026-11-12: a large git diff hung the app).
+ *   - what the call hands back is the first 200 KB, and SAYS it was cut, with the whole size and how to ask for less
+ *   - the live view gets at most ~256 KB of it, then one line saying the rest is not shown as it runs
+ *   - a small output is untouched: no note, every chunk live
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-big-process-output.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const { execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) { if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); } }
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bigout-'));
+try {
+  const run = async (code) => {
+    const live = [];
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const r = extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, { cwd: proj, projectDir: proj, onProgress: (s) => live.push(String(s)) }));
+    return { r, live: live.join(''), ms: Date.now() - t0 };
+  };
+
+  console.log('\n[1] three MB on stdout');
+  // (3 MB in 64 KB writes: node -e, so it runs the same everywhere)
+  const big = await run(`const r = await node(["-e", "const l = 'x'.repeat(1023) + String.fromCharCode(10); for (let i = 0; i < 3072; i++) process.stdout.write(l)"]); return { len: r.stdout.length, tail: r.stdout.slice(-400) };`);
+  const m = /"len":\s*(\d+)/.exec(big.r);
+  const len = m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
+  ok(len > 200 * 1024 && len < 201 * 1024, `the result is the first 200 KB and the note (${len} chars)`);
+  ok(/stdout CUT: it was 3\.0 MB/.test(big.r), 'and says it was cut, and how big the whole was');
+  ok(/narrower command, or send the output to a file/.test(big.r), 'and how to ask for less');
+  ok(big.live.length <= 260 * 1024, `the live view got at most ~256 KB (${Math.round(big.live.length / 1024)} KB)`);
+  ok(/the rest of the output is not shown here as it runs/.test(big.live), 'and one line saying the rest is not shown');
+
+  ok(!/git diff --stat/.test(big.r), '(the git advice is for git only)');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a big git diff says how to narrow a diff');
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'a.txt'), 'a line\n'.repeat(40000));
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'b.txt'), 'b line\n'.repeat(40000));
+  const g = await run(`const r = await git(["diff", "--no-index", "a.txt", "b.txt"], { allowFail: true }); return r.stdout.slice(-400);`);
+  ok(/stdout CUT: it was \d+ KB/.test(g.r) && /git diff --stat \(or --name-only\) first, then one path or a commit range/.test(g.r), `git: --stat first, then a path or a range (${g.r.slice(-160).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a small output is untouched');
+  const small = await run(`const r = await node(["-e", "console.log('hello there')"]); return r.stdout;`);
+  ok(/hello there/.test(small.r) && !/CUT/.test(small.r), 'no note on a small output');
+  ok(/hello there/.test(small.live) && !/not shown here/.test(small.live), 'and all of it live');
+} finally {
+  await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * WHERE THE CONVERSATION STAYS (the developer, 2026-11-12: "if I resize the middle area or sometimes when new messages
+ * come it scrolls me up in the conversation. On resizing, if the view is pinned to the bottom, it should stay on the
+ * bottom. If new messages come, it shouldn't scroll me unless I'm pinned to the bottom, then it should scroll down to the
+ * new content"). Through the real page:
+ *   [1] pinned to the bottom: narrower and wider, it stays at the bottom; a message that grows as it streams, too
+ *   [2] scrolled up (the wheel): a new message below does not move the view; the arrow says there is more
+ *   [3] scrolled up: rows added ABOVE the view, a row above growing, and a resize - the row at the top stays put
+ *   [4] back at the bottom (the arrow): pinned again, and the next message is followed
+ * Run: node test/test-chat-scroll-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

+if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP - Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };

+const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });

+  const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+  await page.goto(`http://localhost:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+  await sleep(500);
+  // a long conversation: rows that wrap (so a width change changes their height)
+  const LONG = 'This is a reply long enough to wrap across several lines when the chat column is narrow, and fewer when it is wide. '.repeat(3);
+  await page.evaluate((t) => { for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'row assistant'; d.dataset.n = String(i); d.innerHTML = `<div class="body">#${i} ${t}</div>`; RC.log.appendChild(d); } jumpToBottom(); }, LONG);
+  await sleep(200);
+  const st = () => page.evaluate(() => { const s = document.getElementById('logScroll'); return { gap: Math.round(s.scrollHeight - s.scrollTop - s.clientHeight), top: s.scrollTop, stick: _stickBottom, arrow: document.getElementById('scrollBottomBtn')?.classList.contains('show') }; });
+  const topRow = () => page.evaluate(() => { const s = document.getElementById('logScroll'), t = s.getBoundingClientRect().top; for (const k of RC.log.children) { const r = k.getBoundingClientRect(); if (r.height && r.bottom > t + 1) return { n: k.dataset.n || k.className, y: Math.round(r.top - t) }; } return null; });
+  const add = (where, text = 'a new message') => page.evaluate((w, x) => { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'row assistant'; d.dataset.n = w + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6); d.innerHTML = `<div class="body">${x}</div>`; if (w === 'top') RC.log.prepend(d); else { RC.log.appendChild(d); scrollToBottom(); } return d.dataset.n; }, where, text);
+
+  console.log('\n[1] pinned to the bottom');
+  let s = await st();
+  ok(s.gap <= 2 && s.stick, `at the bottom to start (${s.gap}px)`);
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 900, height: 900 }); await sleep(400);
+  s = await st();
+  ok(s.gap <= 2 && s.stick, `narrower (the lines re-wrap, the log grows): still at the bottom (${s.gap}px from it)`);
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1600, height: 700 }); await sleep(400);
+  s = await st();
+  ok(s.gap <= 2 && s.stick, `wider and shorter: still at the bottom (${s.gap}px)`);
+  await page.evaluate((t) => { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'row assistant'; d.id = 'streaming'; d.innerHTML = '<div class="body">streaming</div>'; RC.log.appendChild(d); scrollToBottom(); }, LONG);

+  await sleep(200);

+/**
+ * THE CHEAT SHEET (/cheatsheet; the developer, 2026-11-12). Through the real page, with a plugin that adds to it:
+ *   [1] /cheatsheet opens on Prompts: code_boss's prompts and the plugin's, grouped by header (the plugin's "Git" item
+ *       in code_boss's Git group, its "Jira" header a group of its own)
+ *   [2] a click shows the prompt as a code block, ⧉ at its upper left; no "Put in the box"
+ *   [3] the filter matches keywords too (never shown), opening a match not in the title or line
+ *   [2b] a <...> in a prompt: marked, without the brackets, and copied without them
+ *   [4] Tour: one tab, the three tours each explained with a Start. The screen: a tour over the live page - the mouse
+ *       over a part outlines it and says what it is; a plugin's tab and button say their own help; a memory item by
+ *       its kind, a process that ended; the bar drags; Esc ends it and brings the sheet back
+ *   [5] Settings: the window opens under the tour, its tabs work through it, a row says what it is
+ *   [6] Branch window: the window a branch name opens, toured
+ *   [7] Behaviors: one played in the real chat view, in its own window, as a list of steps - a step clicked shows the
+ *       chat just before it (the steer waiting, greyed, under the working line), Play goes on from there; Stop's
+ *       message out of the chat and back in the box; the main conversation untouched
+ *   [8] Esc closes the sheet; no page errors
+ * Run: node test/test-cheatsheet-e2e.mjs

+/**
+ * THE BRANCH WINDOW: WHAT A PUSH WOULD SEND, end to end through the real page (the developer, 2026-11-12: "our commit
+ * screen is not really what we need ... the agent already auto-commits ... see the local commits and how they differ from
+ * the remote repo ... as well as uncommitted changes ... For each commit and each file, there should be a button that
+ * closes the modal and puts a reference to that commit or commit/file at the bottom of the text input box").
+ *   [1] a branch badge opens it: what is not committed yet, then the commits the remote does not have, newest first;
+ *       the line on top says the branch, its upstream, how many to push and how many to pull
+ *   [2] a commit opens to its files; a file to its diff; a CRLF re-save says "line endings only"
+ *   [3] ↳ on a commit: the window closes, a reference to the commit is at the end of the message box
+ *   [4] ↳ on a file of a commit, and on a file not committed: each adds its own line, after what was typed
+ *   [5] another repo's window shows that repo; one not on a remote says so; one the same as the remote says so
+ *   [6] copy: Markdown with what is not committed and each commit's diffs; no commit-suggestion call is ever made
+ * Run: node test/test-commit-modal-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+const modelCalls = [];
+const adapter = {
+  async complete({ messages }) { modelCalls.push((messages || []).map((m) => m.content).join('\n').slice(0, 200)); return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cmodal-'));
+const git = (dir, cmd) => execSync('git ' + cmd, { cwd: dir, stdio: 'pipe' }).toString();
+const repoA = path.join(proj, 'alpha'), repoB = path.join(proj, 'beta'), repoC = path.join(proj, 'gamma');
+const bareA = path.join(proj, '.remotes', 'alpha.git'), bareC = path.join(proj, '.remotes', 'gamma.git');
+await mkdir(path.dirname(bareA), { recursive: true });
+for (const b of [bareA, bareC]) git(proj, `init -q --bare "${b}"`);
+for (const [r, bare] of [[repoA, bareA], [repoB, null], [repoC, bareC]]) {
+  await mkdir(r, { recursive: true });
+  git(r, 'init -q -b main'); git(r, 'config user.email t@t'); git(r, 'config user.name "Tess Dev"'); git(r, 'config commit.gpgsign false'); git(r, 'config core.autocrlf false');
+  await writeFile(path.join(r, 'app.js'), Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => `app line ${i + 1}`).join('\n') + '\n');
+  git(r, 'add -A'); git(r, 'commit -qm start');
+  if (bare) { git(r, `remote add origin "${bare}"`); git(r, 'push -q -u origin main'); }

=== add test/test-deploy-plugin-race.mjs
new c57f5d84005759032a6260c3
+/**
+ * deployPlugin and the reload already running (the dev agent on prod, build 2810: "still loading after 8s" on every
+ * deploy, then EPERM when it deployed again while the reload was still reading the folder):
+ *   [1] deployPlugin waits for the reloads already queued before it writes into the folder
+ *   [2] the manager says where a reload is, and whenIdle() waits for the queue - or says it ran out of time
+ * Run: node test/test-deploy-plugin-race.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-deprace-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+const { createPluginManager } = await import('../src/plugins/index.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] the deploy waits for the reload already running');
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir();
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-deprace-proj-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'mine__2.mjs'), 'export default { description: "mine", tools: [] };');
+  const order = [];
+  let idleAt = 0;
+  const report = async (abs) => { order.push('report:' + (existsSync(abs) ? 'written' : 'missing')); return { failed: false, text: 'plugin mine v2 loaded: no tools' }; };
+  report.idle = async (ms) => {
+    order.push('idle:' + ms + ':' + (existsSync(path.join(pdir, 'mine__2.mjs')) ? 'written' : 'not-yet'));
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300)); idleAt = Date.now(); return true;
+  };
+  let i = 0; const events = [];
+  const replies = ['<remote-workspace><js>return JSON.stringify(await deployPlugin({ file: "mine__2.mjs" }));</js></remote-workspace>', 'Done.\n/--- you ab12 ---'];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)] } }] }; } };
+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: proj,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {},
+    pluginWriteReport: report });
+  const res = String(events.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval')?.content || '');
+  ok(order[0] === 'idle:60000:not-yet', `it waits for the queued reloads first, before the file is in the folder (${order.join(' > ')})`);
+  ok(order[1] === 'report:written' && idleAt > 0, 'then writes, then waits for its own reload');
+  ok(/"ok":true/.test(res) && /loaded/.test(res), `and the outcome comes back (${res.slice(0, 120)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the manager: where a reload is, and a bounded wait');
+  const dir2 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-deprace-pl-'));
+  const pm = createPluginManager({ log: () => {} });
+  pm.setPluginsDir(dir2);
+  ok(typeof pm.reconcileStep === 'function' && pm.reconcileStep() === 'idle', `idle when nothing runs (${pm.reconcileStep()})`);
+  // a plugin whose init takes a while holds a reload open
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir2, 'slow__1.mjs'), 'export default { description: "slow", tools: [], async init() { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 700)); } };');
+  const run = pm.reconcile();
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
+  const mid = pm.reconcileStep();
+  ok(/slow/.test(mid), `mid-reload it names the step (${mid})`);
+  ok((await pm.whenIdle(50)) === false, 'a short wait runs out and says so');
+  ok((await pm.whenIdle(5000)) === true && pm.reconcileStep() === 'idle', 'a longer one sees the reload through');
+  await run;
+  await rm(dir2, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+} finally {
+  await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-git-outgoing.mjs
new 93785af8620f8c49e2e93153
+/**
+ * WHAT A PUSH WOULD SEND (git-commit.mjs collectOutgoingAsync / commitFilesAsync; the developer, 2026-11-12: the branch
+ * window shows the local commits against the remote and what is not committed, not commit suggestions). On real repos:
+ *   [1] a branch with an upstream: the commits it does not have (newest first), how many it has that we do not, the
+ *       uncommitted files and a new file
+ *   [2] a commit's files and their diffs; whitespace-only lines counted - a file re-saved with CRLF is all whitespace
+ *   [3] no upstream but origin/<branch> exists: compared with that; no remote at all: the last commits, and it says so
+ *   [4] THE CONTAINER'S GIT IS JGIT: every command the read uses, run through our JGit command line and through git on
+ *       the same repo - the same answer (prod, 2026-10-30: one flag JGit refused emptied the commit window for everyone)
+ * Run: node test/test-git-outgoing.mjs
+ */
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { collectOutgoingAsync, commitFilesAsync, gcWhitespaceOnlyLines, parseDiffFiles } from '../src/agent/git-commit.mjs';
+import { jgitLib, jgitBuild } from './_jgit-lib.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const work = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-outgoing-'));
+const HOME = path.join(work, 'home');
+mkdirSync(HOME, { recursive: true });
+writeFileSync(path.join(HOME, '.gitconfig'), '[user]\n\tname = Test Dev\n\temail = dev@example.com\n[init]\n\tdefaultBranch = main\n[core]\n\tautocrlf = false\n');
+Object.assign(process.env, { HOME, USERPROFILE: HOME, GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: '1', GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT: '0' });
+const g = (cwd, ...args) => { const r = spawnSync('git', args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8' }); if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(' ')}: ${r.stderr}`); return r.stdout; };
+const W = (dir, f, text) => { mkdirSync(path.dirname(path.join(dir, f)), { recursive: true }); writeFileSync(path.join(dir, f), text); };
+const lines = (n, tag, eol = '\n') => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => `${tag} line ${i + 1}`).join(eol) + eol;
+
+const BARE = path.join(work, 'origin.git'), R = path.join(work, 'repo'), OTHER = path.join(work, 'other');
+g(work, 'init', '-q', '--bare', BARE);
+g(work, 'clone', '-q', BARE, R);
+W(R, 'app.js', lines(30, 'app'));
+W(R, 'util.js', 'export const one = 1;\n');
+g(R, 'add', '-A'); g(R, 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'start'); g(R, 'push', '-q', '-u', 'origin', 'main');
+// the remote moves on (someone else)
+g(work, 'clone', '-q', BARE, OTHER); W(OTHER, 'theirs.txt', 'theirs\n'); g(OTHER, 'add', '-A'); g(OTHER, 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'their change'); g(OTHER, 'push', '-q');
+g(R, 'fetch', '-q');
+// two local commits: a real change, then a file re-saved with CRLF and one line changed
+W(R, 'util.js', 'export const one = 1;\nexport const two = 2;\n');
+g(R, 'add', '-A'); g(R, 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'add two');
+W(R, 'app.js', lines(30, 'app', '\r\n').replace('app line 7\r\n', 'app line seven\r\n'));
+g(R, 'add', '-A'); g(R, 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'app: line seven');
+// not committed: a change and a new file
+W(R, 'util.js', 'export const one = 1;\nexport const two = 2;\nexport const three = 3;\n');
+W(R, 'notes.md', 'new notes\n');
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] a branch with an upstream');
+  const o = await collectOutgoingAsync(R);
+  ok(o.branch === 'main' && o.upstream === 'origin/main' && !o.noRemote, `the branch and its upstream (${o.branch} -> ${o.upstream})`);
+  ok(o.commits.length === 2 && o.commits[0].subject === 'app: line seven' && o.commits[1].subject === 'add two' && /^[0-9a-f]{7,}$/.test(o.commits[0].short) && o.commits[0].author === 'Test Dev' && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(o.commits[0].at)), `the two commits the remote does not have, newest first (${o.commits.map((c) => c.subject).join(' | ')})`);
+  ok(o.ahead === 2 && o.behind === 1, `ahead 2, and the remote has 1 we have not pulled (${o.ahead}/${o.behind})`);
+  const uf = o.uncommitted.files.map((f) => f.path).sort();
+  ok(uf.join(',') === 'util.js' && o.uncommitted.untracked.join(',') === 'notes.md', `not committed: util.js changed, notes.md new (${uf.join(',')} + ${o.uncommitted.untracked.join(',')})`);
+  ok(o.uncommitted.files[0].adds === 1 && o.uncommitted.files[0].dels === 0 && o.uncommitted.files[0].wsOnly === 0, 'its count: one line added, no whitespace-only lines');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a commit\'s files');
+  const f1 = await commitFilesAsync(R, o.commits[1].sha);
+  ok(f1 && f1.length === 1 && f1[0].path === 'util.js' && f1[0].adds === 1 && f1[0].hunks[0].lines.some((l) => l === '+export const two = 2;'), `"add two": util.js, +1, its diff (${f1 && f1.map((f) => f.path)})`);
+  const f0 = await commitFilesAsync(R, o.commits[0].sha);
+  ok(f0 && f0[0].path === 'app.js' && f0[0].adds === 30 && f0[0].dels === 30 && f0[0].wsOnly === 29 && f0[0].eolOnly === 29, `the CRLF re-save: 30 lines changed, 29 of them line endings only (${f0 && f0[0].adds}/${f0 && f0[0].wsOnly}/${f0 && f0[0].eolOnly})`);
+  ok(await commitFilesAsync(R, 'not-a-sha') === null, 'a bad id is refused, not passed to git');
+  ok(gcWhitespaceOnlyLines(parseDiffFiles('diff --git a/x b/x\n--- a/x\n+++ b/x\n@@ -1,2 +1,2 @@\n-  a = 1\n-b\n+a = 1\n+c\n')[0]) === 1, 'whitespace-only: re-indented counts, a real change does not');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] other remotes');
+  g(R, 'checkout', '-q', '-b', 'feature');
+  g(R, 'stash', '-q', '-u'); W(R, 'f.txt', 'f\n'); g(R, 'add', '-A'); g(R, 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'feature work');
+  g(R, 'push', '-q', 'origin', 'feature');   // pushed with no -u: no upstream configured
+  W(R, 'f.txt', 'f\nmore\n'); g(R, 'add', '-A'); g(R, 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'more feature work');
+  const o2 = await collectOutgoingAsync(R);
+  ok(o2.upstream === 'origin/feature' && o2.commits.length === 1 && o2.commits[0].subject === 'more feature work', `no upstream set, origin/feature there: compared with it (${o2.upstream}, ${o2.commits.length})`);
+  g(R, 'checkout', '-q', '-b', 'local-only');
+  W(R, 'l.txt', 'l\n'); g(R, 'add', '-A'); g(R, 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'local only');
+  const o3 = await collectOutgoingAsync(R);
+  ok(o3.noRemote === true && o3.upstream === null && o3.ahead === null && o3.commits[0].subject === 'local only' && o3.commits.length >= 4, `not on a remote: its last commits, and noRemote (${o3.commits.length})`);
+  g(R, 'checkout', '-q', 'main'); g(R, 'stash', 'pop', '-q');
+
+  console.log('\n[4] the container\'s git (JGit) gives the same answers');
+  const lib = jgitLib(), J = lib && jgitBuild(work, lib);
+  if (!J) console.log('  SKIP: no JGit jars here (test/_jgit-lib.mjs)');
+  else {
+    const jg = (args) => spawnSync(J.java, ['-Duser.home=' + HOME, '-cp', J.cp, 'cb.jgit.Git', ...args], { cwd: R, encoding: 'utf8' });
+    const rg = (args) => spawnSync('git', args, { cwd: R, encoding: 'utf8' });
+    const sha = o.commits[0].sha;
+    const cmds = [
+      ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'],
+      ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', '--symbolic-full-name', '@{u}'],
+      ['rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', 'refs/remotes/origin/main'],
+      ['log', '--max-count=101', '--format=%H%x00%h%x00%s%x00%an%x00%aI%x1e', 'origin/main..HEAD'],
+      ['rev-list', '--count', 'HEAD..origin/main'],
+      ['show', '--format=', '-p', sha],
+      ['diff', 'HEAD', '--', '.', ':(exclude).code_boss'],
+      ['diff', '-w', '--stat', 'HEAD~1', 'HEAD'],
+    ];
+    for (const c of cmds) {
+      const a = rg(c), b = jg(c);
+      const norm = (s) => String(s || '').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/^index [0-9a-f]+\.\.[0-9a-f]+( \d+)?$/gm, 'index').trimEnd();
+      // (show: JGit prints two empty lines before the diff - read through parseDiffFiles, as the window reads it, they are the same)
+      if (c[0] === 'show') { const pa = JSON.stringify(parseDiffFiles(a.stdout)), pb = JSON.stringify(parseDiffFiles(b.stdout)); ok(a.status === 0 && b.status === 0 && pa === pb, `${c.join(' ').slice(0, 50)}: the same files and hunks as read (${b.status === 0 ? (pa === pb ? 'the same' : 'DIFFERENT') : 'JGit refused'})`); continue; }
+      ok(a.status === 0 && b.status === 0 && norm(a.stdout) === norm(b.stdout), `${c.join(' ').slice(0, 70)}: ${b.status === 0 ? (norm(a.stdout) === norm(b.stdout) ? +/**
+ * THE INSPECTOR CAN READ ANY CALL, HOWEVER OLD, AND HOW IT STREAMED (the developer, 2026-11-12: "the inspector said that's
+ * all the messages it can see. Why put a limit on how far it can go back?"; "the inspector says it can't see the stream
+ * marker, let's change that"; "Why not just have them roll off into an archive and allow the Inspector to search back in
+ * the archives?"). Through the real server, with the raw log's rotation made tiny so the first calls roll off:
+ *   [1] the old generations are in raw-log-archive/, compressed (Brotli)
+ *   [2] the first call - long gone from raw-log.jsonl - is read whole from the archive: its request, its response
+ *   [3] its stream: each chunk, its offset and length, the text either side of each boundary; the marks
+ *   [4] the page's raw view finds it in the archive too
+ *   [5] Clear: a call from before it is still found (the raw log went into the archive); Reset: the log, the archive and the
+ *       kept answers go
+ *   [6] (a run of its own, the archive capped tiny) the oldest archives are removed; an old call's request is then said
+ *       to be gone, and its answer and stream are still read from raw-calls.jsonl
+ * Run: node test/test-inspector-raw-kept.mjs
+ */

+  async *completeStream(req) {

+    const before = archived().length;

+    for (let k = 0; k < 40 && archived().some((f) => /\.(part|tmp)$/.test(f)); k++) await sleep(150);

+/**
+ * THE AGENT'S BROWSER IN CODE_BOSS'S WINDOW - stage 2, the manager's broker (PanelBroker.java), against the real thing:
+ * code_boss's real page in a real Chrome (opened with its DevTools port, as App.openWindow does with the setting on),
+ * the broker compiled from the launcher's sources, and a raw DevTools client that connects THE WAY THE PROD PLUGIN DOES
+ * (chrome_dev_debug, 2026-11-12): /json/version -> webSocketDebuggerUrl -> Target.createTarget / attachToTarget(flatten)
+ * / getTargets / closeTarget, and the rest on the page's session.
+ *   [1] the browser endpoint: /json/version names the broker's own ws address; a web page (Origin) is refused
+ *   [2] one page: getTargets lists the panel only; createTarget puts the app in the panel (localhost -> 127.0.0.1)
+ *   [3] the session: the enables the plugin sends, Runtime.evaluate, addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument, lifecycle and
+ *       console events, a trusted click and typed text, Network events and getResponseBody, the plugin's own Fetch
+ *   [4] a dialog in the page reaches the plugin on its session and is answered there
+ *   [5] setDeviceMetricsOverride sizes the page (Chrome refuses it on an iframe; the broker sizes the iframe)
+ *   [6] a page that refuses to be framed (X-Frame-Options + CSP frame-ancestors) is shown anyway
+ *   [7] Page.navigate to localhost stays on the panel's own site; a cross-site navigation keeps the session
+ *   [8] nothing past the page: Browser.*, Target.* / Storage.* on the session, another target id - refused
+ *   [9] closeTarget blanks the panel; the address line follows the agent's navigations
+ *   [10] an app that loads other apps in frames: the panel's frame is the TOP frame to the plugin (no parentId); frames
+ *        at the app's own address (relative, or absolute localhost) are searchable from the page; a frame on another
+ *        site is reached as in any Chrome - Target.setAutoAttach on the page's session
+ * Needs Windows, a JDK and Chrome. Run: node test/test-panel-broker-e2e.mjs

+
+// ── a raw DevTools client, as the plugin has one (no Origin, flatten sessions) ──
+function connect(url, headers = {}) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const u = new URL(url);
+    const s = net.connect(+u.port, u.hostname);
+    const key = Buffer.from(String(Math.random())).toString('base64');
+    s.write(`GET ${u.pathname} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: ${u.host}\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Key: ${key}\r\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n${Object.entries(headers).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}\r\n`).join('')}\r\n`);
+    let buf = Buffer.alloc(0), open = false;
+    const c = { seq: 0, waiting: new Map(), events: [], closed: false };
+    c.send = (method, params = {}, sessionId) => new Promise((r) => { const id = ++c.seq; c.waiting.set(id, r); const p = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params, ...(sessionId ? { sessionId } : {}) })); const mask = Buffer.from([1, 2, 3, 4]); let h; if (p.length < 126) h = Buffer.from([0x81, 0x80 | p.length]); else if (p.length < 65536) { h = Buffer.alloc(4); h[0] = 0x81; h[1] = 0x80 | 126; h.writeUInt16BE(p.length, 2); } else { h = Buffer.alloc(10); h[0] = 0x81; h[1] = 0x80 | 127; h.writeBigUInt64BE(BigInt(p.length), 2); } const q = Buffer.from(p.map((b, i) => b ^ mask[i & 3])); s.write(Buffer.concat([h, mask, q])); });
+    c.ev = async (pred, ms = 6000) => { const t = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t < ms) { const i = c.events.findIndex(pred); if (i >= 0) return c.events.splice(i, 1)[0]; await sleep(40); } return null; };
+    c.close = () => s.destroy();
+    s.on('data', (d) => {
+      buf = Buffer.concat([buf, d]);
+      if (!open) { const i = buf.indexOf('\r\n\r\n'); if (i < 0) return; const head = buf.slice(0, i).toString(); buf = buf.slice(i + 4); if (!/^HTTP\/1\.1 101/.test(head)) { s.destroy(); reject(new Error(head.split('\r\n')[0])); return; } open = true; resolve(c); }
+      for (;;) {
+        if (buf.length < 2) return;
+        let len = buf[1] & 0x7f, off = 2;
+        if (len === 126) { if (buf.length < 4) return; len = buf.readUInt16BE(2); off = 4; } else if (len === 127) { if (buf.length < 10) return; len = Number(buf.readBigUInt64BE(2)); off = 10; }
+        if (buf.length < off + len) return;
+        const m = JSON.parse(buf.slice(off, off + len).toString()); buf = buf.slice(off + len);
+        if (m.id && c.waiting.has(m.id)) { c.waiting.get(m.id)(m); c.waiting.delete(m.id); } else c.events.push(m);
+      }
+    });
+    s.on('error', reject); s.on('close', () => { c.closed = true; for (const [, r] of c.waiting) r({ error: { message: 'the connection closed' } }); c.waiting.clear(); });
+  });
+}
+const getText = (p, headers = {}) => new Promise((r) => http.get({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: +BP, path: p, headers }, (res) => { let b = ''; res.on('data', (d) => b += d); res.on('end', () => r({ status: res.statusCode, body: b })); }).on('error', (e) => r({ status: 0, body: String(e) })));
+const val = (r) => r?.result?.result?.value;

+  const fromPage = await getText('/json/version', { Origin: 'http://evil.example' });

+  ok(fromPage.status === 403 && wsRefused, 'a web page (an Origin) is refused, on HTTP and on the WebSocket');
+
+  const c = await connect(ver.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
+  console.log('\n[2] one page, the panel');
+  const t0 = (await c.send('Target.getTargets')).result.targetInfos;
+  ok(t0.length === 1 && t0[0].targetId === TID && t0[0].type === 'page', `getTargets: the panel only (${t0.map((t) => t.type + ' ' + t.url).join(', ')})`);
+  const ct = await c.send('Target.createTarget', { url: `http://localhost:${APP}/start` });

+  const at = await c.send('Target.attachToTarget', { targetId: TID, flatten: true });

+  ok(!!S && attachedEv?.params?.sessionId === S, `attachToTarget(flatten): a session, and its attachedToTarget event (${S})`);
+  // what the developer sees: code_boss's window, through its real DevTools port (the test looks; the plugin cannot)
+  const { default: puppeteer } = await import('puppeteer-core');
+  const portFile = path.join(profileBase, 'chrome-container-' + CB_PORT, 'DevToolsActivePort');
+  const [cport] = (await readFile(portFile, 'utf8')).split(/\r?\n/);
+  const real = await puppeteer.connect({ browserURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:' + cport, defaultViewport: null });

+  ok(seen.view && /^http:\/\/localhost:\d+\/start$/.test(seen.src) && seen.w >= 1280, `the developer sees it: the panel's browser view is open on the app (${JSON.stringify(seen)})`);
+  const st = await c.send('Panel.state', {}, undefined);   // (nothing: unknown at the browser level)
+  ok(st.error && /not available/.test(st.error.message), 'an unknown browser-level call is a sentence, not silence');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the session, as the plugin uses it');
+  const enables = ['Page.enable', 'Runtime.enable', 'Log.enable', 'Network.enable', 'DOM.enable'];
+  const er = await Promise.all(enables.map((m) => c.send(m, {}, S)));
+  ok(er.every((r) => !r.error), 'Page/Runtime/Log/Network/DOM.enable');
+  ok(!(await c.send('Page.setLifecycleEventsEnabled', { enabled: true }, S)).error && !(await c.send('Network.setCacheDisabled', { cacheDisabled: true }, S)).error, 'setLifecycleEventsEnabled, setCacheDisabled');
+  ok(!(await c.send('Page.addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument', { source: 'window.__plugin = 42' }, S)).error, 'addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument');
+  c.events.length = 0;
+  const nav = await c.send('Page.navigate', { url: `http://localhost:${APP}/again` }, S);
+  const life = await c.ev((e) => e.sessionId === S && e.method === 'Page.lifecycleEvent' && e.params.name === 'load', 8000);
+  ok(!nav.error && !!life, 'Page.navigate, and the load lifecycle event on the session');
+  const where = val(await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'location.href + " " + window.__plugin + " " + document.title', returnByValue: true }, S));
+  ok(/^http:\/\/localhost:\d+\/again 42 THE APP$/.test(where || ''), `the app keeps its own address (localhost); the plugin's script ran (${where})`);
+  const cons = await c.ev((e) => e.sessionId === S && e.method === 'Runtime.consoleAPICalled', 4000);
+  ok(cons?.params?.args?.[0]?.value === 'app says hi', 'Runtime.consoleAPICalled reaches the plugin');
+  const dataReq = await c.ev((e) => e.sessionId === S && e.method === 'Network.responseReceived' && /\/data$/.test(e.params.response.url), 4000);
+  await c.ev((e) => e.sessionId === S && e.method === 'Network.loadingFinished' && e.params.requestId === dataReq?.params?.requestId, 3000);
+  const body = dataReq && await c.send('Network.getResponseBody', { requestId: dataReq.params.requestId }, S);
+  ok(body?.result?.body === '{"the":"data"}', `Network events and getResponseBody (${body?.result?.body || JSON.stringify(body?.error)})`);
+  for (const type of ['mousePressed', 'mouseReleased']) await c.send('Input.dispatchMouseEvent', { type, x: 400, y: 240, button: 'left', clickCount: 1 }, S);
+  await sleep(200);
+  const clicked = val(await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'document.title', returnByValue: true }, S));
+  await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'document.getElementById("t").focus()' }, S);
+  await c.send('Input.insertText', { text: 'typed by the agent' }, S);
+  const typed = val(await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'document.getElementById("t").value', returnByValue: true }, S));
+  if (clicked !== 'clicked') console.log('   ', JSON.stringify(await win.evaluate(() => { const v = document.getElementById('rpBrowser'), f = document.getElementById('agentFrame'); return { view: v.className, vw: v.offsetWidth, w: f.offsetWidth, h: f.offsetHeight, k: f.style.transform, right: document.getElementById('rightPanel').className, tab: document.getElementById('rpTabBrowser').className }; })));
+  ok(clicked === 'clicked' && typed === 'typed by the agent', `a click at the page's own coordinates (${clicked}) and typed text (${typed}), through the panel's scaling`);
+  await win.evaluate(() => rpSelect('running'));
+  await sleep(300);
+  await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'document.title = "THE APP"' }, S);
+  for (const type of ['mousePressed', 'mouseReleased']) await c.send('Input.dispatchMouseEvent', { type, x: 400, y: 240, button: 'left', clickCount: 1 }, S);
+  await sleep(200);
+  const behind = val(await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'document.title + " " + innerWidth', returnByValue: true }, S));
+  ok(/^clicked \d{4}/.test(behind || ''), `with the developer on the running list, the page keeps its size and takes the agent's clicks (${behind})`);
+  await win.evaluate(() => rpSelect('browser'));
+  // the plugin's own Fetch, beside the broker's
+  ok(!(await c.send('Fetch.enable', { patterns: [{ urlPattern: '*/data*' }] }, S)).error, 'the plugin enables Fetch');
+  c.events.length = 0;
+  c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'fetch("/data?x").then(r=>r.text())', awaitPromise: true, returnByValue: true }, S).then((r) => { c.fetched = val(r); });
+  const paused = await c.ev((e) => e.sessionId === S && e.method === 'Fetch.requestPaused', 5000);
+  if (paused) await c.send('Fetch.fulfillRequest', { requestId: paused.params.requestId, responseCode: 200, responseHeaders: [{ name: 'content-type', value: 'text/plain' }], body: Buffer.from('from the plugin').toString('base64') }, S);
+  await sleep(400);
+  ok(c.fetched === 'from the plugin', `Fetch.requestPaused reaches the plugin, and its fulfillRequest answers the page (${c.fetched})`);
+  await c.send('Fetch.disable', {}, S);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] a dialog');
+  c.events.length = 0;
+  c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'confirm("sure?")', returnByValue: true }, S).then((r) => { c.confirmed = val(r); });
+  const dlg = await c.ev((e) => e.method === 'Page.javascriptDialogOpening', 5000);
+  ok(dlg?.sessionId === S && dlg?.params?.message === 'sure?', `javascriptDialogOpening on the plugin's session (${dlg?.sessionId === S ? 'its session' : dlg?.sessionId})`);
+  const hd = await c.send('Page.handleJavaScriptDialog', { accept: true }, S);
+  await sleep(300);
+  ok(!hd.error && c.confirmed === true, `handleJavaScriptDialog on its session answers it (confirm -> ${c.confirmed})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[5] the page\'s size');
+  ok(!(await c.send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 1024, height: 700, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: false }, S)).error, 'setDeviceMetricsOverride is taken');
+  await sleep(500);

+  ok(size === '1024x700', `the page is laid out at 1024x700 (${size})`);
+  await c.send('Emulation.clearDeviceMetricsOverride', {}, S);

+  ok(size2 >= 1280, `cleared: a desktop width again (${size2})`);

+  console.log('\n[6] a page that refuses to be framed');

+  await c.ev((e) => e.sessionId === S && e.method === 'Page.lifecycleEvent' && e.params.name === 'load', 8000);

+  ok(!other.error && !c.events.some((e) => e.method === 'Target.detachedFromTarget'), `a cross-site navigation keeps the session (${val(other) || other.error?.message})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[8] nothing past the page');

+  const r2 = await c.send('Target.getTargets', {}, S);

+  const r4 = await c.send('Target.attachToTarget', { targetId: 'A'.repeat(32), flatten: true });
+  const r5 = await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: '1' }, 'NOT-A-SESSION');
+  ok([r1, r2, r3, r4, r5].every((r) => r.error), `refused: Browser.close (${r1.error?.message?.slice(0, 50)}...), Target.* and Storage.* on the session, another target, an unknown session`);
+  const top = val(await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'try { window.top.location.href } catch (e) { "blocked: " + e.name }', returnByValue: true }, S));

+  await c.send('Page.enable', {}, S2);

+  if (w2 !== '/second-round') console.log('   ', JSON.stringify(n2), JSON.stringify(await c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'location.pathname', returnByValue: true }, S2)));
+  ok(!n2.error && w2 === '/second-round', `createTarget(about:blank) -> attach -> navigate again (${w2})`);
+

+  await c.send('Page.enable', {}, S3);
+  c.events.length = 0;

+  const topNav = navs.find((e) => /\/nest$/.test(e.params.frame.url));
+  ok(topNav && !topNav.params.frame.parentId && navs.some((e) => /inner\?rel/.test(e.params.frame.url) && e.params.frame.parentId), `the panel's frame is the top frame (frameNavigated for /nest has no parentId; its inner frames have one)`);

+  ok(/^found rel \| found abs \| other: \w+Error$/.test(found || ''), `searched from the page: the frames at the app's own address (relative and absolute localhost) are found, the other site's is not - as in any Chrome (${found})`);
+  c.events.length = 0;
+  const aa = await c.send('Target.setAutoAttach', { autoAttach: true, waitForDebuggerOnStart: false, flatten: true }, S3);
+  const kid = await c.ev((e) => e.sessionId === S3 && e.method === 'Target.attachedToTarget', 4000);
+  const KS = kid?.params?.sessionId;

+  ok(!aa.error && kid?.params?.targetInfo?.url?.includes('127.0.0.1') && deep === 'found other', `the other site's frame: Target.setAutoAttach on the page's session attaches it, and its session answers (${deep || JSON.stringify(aa.error || kid)})`);
+  const kidRefused = KS && await c.send('Target.getTargets', {}, KS);
+  ok(kidRefused?.error, 'and nothing past it from there either (Target.getTargets on its session is refused)');
+  c.close();
+  real.disconnect();
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }

+/**
+ * THE AGENT'S BROWSER IN CODE_BOSS'S WINDOW, HARDER (the developer, 2026-11-12: "please do more testing on the internal
+ * browser"). The same set-up as test-panel-broker-e2e (the real broker, code_boss's real page in a real Chrome on
+ * codeboss.localhost), and what that one does not do:
+ *   [1] puppeteer as the client (browserWSEndpoint): pages(), newPage, goto, click, type, waitForSelector, frames
+ *   [2] two clients at once: one navigates, the other sees it; one leaves, the other goes on
+ *   [3] the developer reloads code_boss's window mid-session: the plugin's session comes back on the same page
+ *   [4] the developer types an address in the panel: the plugin sees the top frame navigate
+ *   [5] a big response (6 MB) through the broker, whole
+ *   [6] a download lands in the downloads folder; an upload from a local path
+ *   [7] prompt() with text, alert(), and a beforeunload asking on the way out
+ *   [8] churn: 10 x createTarget -> attach -> navigate -> closeTarget; 5 x connect and drop
+ *   [9] the panel collapsed, and fullscreen: the agent's clicks still land
+ *   [10] a page that tries to get out: top navigation, a popup, code_boss's API, the broker's own socket
+ *   [11] code_boss's window closed and opened again (a new Chrome port): the plugin's cached address still works
+ * Needs Windows, a JDK, Chrome and node_modules/puppeteer-core. Run: node test/test-panel-browser-hard-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, existsSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import http from 'node:http';
+import net from 'node:net';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { sblFindJdk, sblWrite, sblCompileArgs, sblJavacMajor } from '../src/agent/sandbox-launcher.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => p && existsSync(p));
+const jdk = sblFindJdk();
+if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !jdk.ok || !chrome || !existsSync(path.join(ROOT, 'node_modules', 'puppeteer-core'))) { console.log('SKIP: needs Windows, a JDK, Chrome and puppeteer-core'); process.exit(0); }
+const { default: puppeteer } = await import('puppeteer-core');
+
+// ── the launcher's classes, and a harness: ONE broker for instance N; "reopen" on its stdin opens the window again ──
+const work = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-pbhard-'));
+const plan = sblWrite(path.join(work, 'launcher'));
+const jv = spawnSync(jdk.javac, ['-version'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+const rel8 = sblJavacMajor(`${jv.stdout} ${jv.stderr}`) >= 9;
+let jc = spawnSync(jdk.javac, sblCompileArgs(plan, { release8: rel8 }), { encoding: 'utf8' });
+if (jc.status !== 0) { console.log('FAIL javac: ' + String(jc.stderr || jc.stdout).slice(0, 800)); process.exit(1); }
+const ID = 60 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20), CB_PORT = 18988 + ID - 1;
+const profileBase = path.join(work, 'profiles');
+mkdirSync(path.join(work, 'harness', 'cb', 'sandbox'), { recursive: true });
+const harness = path.join(work, 'harness', 'cb', 'sandbox', 'PanelBrokerHarness2.java');
+writeFileSync(harness, `package cb.sandbox;
+public class PanelBrokerHarness2 {
+  static Process open(App.Inst i, String exe) throws Exception {
+    java.io.File prof = App.windowProfile(i);
+    prof.mkdirs();
+    new java.io.File(prof, "DevToolsActivePort").delete();
+    java.io.File nul = new java.io.File("NUL");
+    return new ProcessBuilder(exe, "--headless=new", "--remote-debugging-port=0", "--user-data-dir=" + prof.getPath(), "--no-first-run", "--window-size=1400,900", "http://" + App.WINDOW_HOST + ":" + i.port + "/").redirectOutput(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.to(nul)).redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.to(nul)).start();
+  }
+  public static void main(String[] a) throws Exception {
+    Manager.browserProfile = new java.io.File(a[0], "unused").getPath();
+    Container.init(java.nio.file.Paths.get(a[1]), java.nio.file.Paths.get(a[1]));
+    App.Inst i = new App.Inst(Integer.parseInt(a[2]));
+    i.win = open(i, a[3]);
+    i.winPort = true; i.state = "running";
+    PanelBroker.Broker b = new PanelBroker.Broker(i);
+    b.start();
+    System.out.println("BROKER " + b.port + " " + b.browserPath + " " + b.targetId);
+    System.out.flush();
+    java.io.BufferedReader in = new java.io.BufferedReader(new java.io.InputStreamReader(System.in));
+    try {
+      for (String l; (l = in.readLine()) != null; ) {
+        if ("reopen".equals(l.trim())) { i.win.destroyForcibly(); i.win.waitFor(); i.win = open(i, a[3]); System.out.println("REOPENED"); System.out.flush(); }
+      }
+    } finally { b.stop(); i.win.destroyForcibly(); }
+  }
+}
+`);

+// a raw client, as the prod plugin has one
+function connect(url, headers = {}) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const u = new URL(url);
+    const s = net.connect(+u.port, u.hostname);
+    s.write(`GET ${u.pathname} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: ${u.host}\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Key: ${Buffer.from(String(Math.random())).toString('base64')}\r\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n${Object.entries(headers).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}\r\n`).join('')}\r\n`);
+    let buf = Buffer.alloc(0), open = false;
+    const c = { seq: 0, waiting: new Map(), events: [], closed: false };
+    c.send = (method, params = {}, sessionId) => new Promise((r) => { if (c.closed) { r({ error: { message: 'closed' } }); return; } const id = ++c.seq; c.waiting.set(id, r); const p = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params, ...(sessionId ? { sessionId } : {}) })); const mask = Buffer.from([7, 1, 3, 9]); let h; if (p.length < 126) h = Buffer.from([0x81, 0x80 | p.length]); else if (p.length < 65536) { h = Buffer.alloc(4); h[0] = 0x81; h[1] = 0x80 | 126; h.writeUInt16BE(p.length, 2); } else { h = Buffer.alloc(10); h[0] = 0x81; h[1] = 0x80 | 127; h.writeBigUInt64BE(BigInt(p.length), 2); } const q = Buffer.from(p.map((b, i) => b ^ mask[i & 3])); s.write(Buffer.concat([h, mask, q])); });
+    c.ev = async (pred, ms = 6000) => { const t = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t < ms) { const i = c.events.findIndex(pred); if (i >= 0) return c.events.splice(i, 1)[0]; await sleep(40); } return null; };
+    c.close = () => s.destroy();
+    s.on('data', (d) => {
+      buf = Buffer.concat([buf, d]);
+      if (!open) { const i = buf.indexOf('\r\n\r\n'); if (i < 0) return; const head = buf.slice(0, i).toString(); buf = buf.slice(i + 4); if (!/^HTTP\/1\.1 101/.test(head)) { s.destroy(); reject(new Error(head.split('\r\n')[0])); return; } open = true; resolve(c); }
+      for (;;) {
+        if (buf.length < 2) return;
+        let len = buf[1] & 0x7f, off = 2;
+        if (len === 126) { if (buf.length < 4) return; len = buf.readUInt16BE(2); off = 4; } else if (len === 127) { if (buf.length < 10) return; len = Number(buf.readBigUInt64BE(2)); off = 10; }
+        if (buf.length < off + len) return;
+        const m = JSON.parse(buf.slice(off, off + len).toString()); buf = buf.slice(off + len);
+        if (m.id && c.waiting.has(m.id)) { c.waiting.get(m.id)(m); c.waiting.delete(m.id); } else c.events.push(m);
+      }
+    });
+    s.on('error', (e) => { if (!open) reject(e); });
+    s.on('close', () => { c.closed = true; for (const [, r] of c.waiting) r({ error: { message: 'the connection closed' } }); c.waiting.clear(); });
+  });
+}
+const val = (r) => r?.result?.result?.value;
+const ev = (c, S, x) => c.send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: x, returnByValue: true, awaitPromise: true }, S).then(val);

+  await c.send('Page.setLifecycleEventsEnabled', { enabled: true }, S);
+  return { c, S };
+}

+const navigate = async (c, S, url) => { c.events.length = 0; const r = await c.send('Page.navigate', { url }, S); await waitLoad(c, S); return r; };
+

+    // THE AGENT'S BROWSER IN CODE_BOSS'S WINDOW (Settings > Agent's browser; PanelBroker): the setting on through
+    // code_boss's own API, the manager opens the window again with its DevTools port, and a plugin INSIDE asks for a
+    // browser and drives the panel the way the prod plugin does - a raw WebSocket on the address it was given.
+    await mk(path.join(tree, 'home', '.code_boss', 'plugins', 'zqpanel__1.mjs'), `import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import net from 'node:net';
+function cdp(wsUrl) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const u = new URL(wsUrl), s = net.connect(+u.port, u.hostname);
+    s.write('GET ' + u.pathname + ' HTTP/1.1\\r\\nHost: ' + u.host + '\\r\\nUpgrade: websocket\\r\\nConnection: Upgrade\\r\\nSec-WebSocket-Key: dGhlIHNhbXBsZSBub25jZQ==\\r\\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\\r\\n\\r\\n');
+    let buf = Buffer.alloc(0), open = false, seq = 0;
+    const wait = new Map();
+    const c = { close: () => s.destroy(), send: (method, params = {}, sessionId) => new Promise((r) => { const id = ++seq; wait.set(id, r); const p = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params, ...(sessionId ? { sessionId } : {}) })); let h; if (p.length < 126) h = Buffer.from([0x81, 0x80 | p.length]); else { h = Buffer.alloc(4); h[0] = 0x81; h[1] = 0x80 | 126; h.writeUInt16BE(p.length, 2); } s.write(Buffer.concat([h, Buffer.from([0, 0, 0, 0]), p])); }) };
+    s.on('error', reject);
+    s.on('data', (d) => {
+      buf = Buffer.concat([buf, d]);
+      if (!open) { const i = buf.indexOf('\\r\\n\\r\\n'); if (i < 0) return; const head = buf.slice(0, i).toString(); buf = buf.slice(i + 4); if (!head.startsWith('HTTP/1.1 101')) { reject(new Error(head)); return; } open = true; resolve(c); }
+      for (;;) {
+        if (buf.length < 2) return;
+        let len = buf[1] & 0x7f, off = 2;
+        if (len === 126) { if (buf.length < 4) return; len = buf.readUInt16BE(2); off = 4; } else if (len === 127) { if (buf.length < 10) return; len = Number(buf.readBigUInt64BE(2)); off = 10; }
+        if (buf.length < off + len) return;
+        const m = JSON.parse(buf.slice(off, off + len).toString()); buf = buf.slice(off + len);

+      }
+    });
+  });

+/**
+ * A SEND HAS NO TIME LIMIT (prod, 2026-11-12: "Send failed - the server did not answer within 15 seconds" while the run
+ * it started went on, and "continue" was put back in the box, to be sent twice; the developer: "why stop retrying and
+ * timeout ever?"). Through the real server and page:
+ *   [1] the server answers after several seconds: a toast says it is waiting, no error, the box stays empty, sent once
+ *   [2] the request fails but the server has the message (its echo came): nothing put back, no error
+ *   [3] the request fails and the server never had it: the text is back in the box, and the error
+ * Run: node test/test-send-timeout-e2e.mjs

+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';

+const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });

+  await sleep(600);

(document.getElementById('modalTitle') || {}).textContent : '', users: [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].map((r) => (r.firstElementChild || r).textContent.replace(/⧉/g, '').trim()) }));
+
+  console.log('\n[1] the server answers after several seconds');
+  // the POST goes to the server at once; its answer reaches the page 4 s later (a busy server)
+  await page.evaluate(() => {
+    const real = window.fetch;
+    window.__cbSendSlowMs = 800;
+    window.fetch = (url, opts) => {
+      if (!String(url).includes('/api/message')) return real(url, opts);
+      if (window.__mode === 'slow') return real(url, opts).then((r) => new Promise((res) => setTimeout(() => res(r), 4000)));
+      if (window.__mode === 'in-then-fail') return real(url, opts).then(() => new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new TypeError('Failed to fetch')), 1200)));
+      return Promise.reject(new TypeError('Failed to fetch'));   // 'lost': never reached the server
+    };
+    window.__mode = 'slow';
+  });
+  await page.focus('#input'); await page.keyboard.type('continue'); await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  await sleep(1500);

+  ok(/slow to answer - your message is waiting/.test(toast) && !u.err && u.box === '', `while it waits: "${toast.replace(/×$/, '')}", no error, the box empty`);
+  await sleep(4000);
+  u = await ui();
+  ok(!u.err && u.box === '' && u.users.filter((t) => t === 'continue').length === 1 && sends === 1, `it answered: no error, the message once in the chat, the model asked once (${JSON.stringify(u)}, ${sends})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the request fails, but the server has the message');
+  await page.evaluate(() => { window.__mode = 'in-then-fail'; });
+  await page.focus('#input'); await page.keyboard.type('second'); await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  await sleep(2500);
+  u = await ui();
+  ok(!u.err && u.box === '' && u.users.includes('second'), `its echo came: nothing put back, no error (${JSON.stringify(u)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the request fails and the server never had it');
+  await page.evaluate(() => { window.__mode = 'lost'; });
+  await page.focus('#input'); await page.keyboard.type('lost one'); await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  await sleep(800);
+  u = await ui();
+  ok(u.box === 'lost one' && /Send failed/.test(u.err) && !u.users.includes('lost one'), `the text is back in the box, and the error (${JSON.stringify(u)})`);
+  ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await browser.close(); server.close(); }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

=== add test/test-thought-cut-unit.mjs
new e3c46c8e801605bf4659a71b
+/**
+ * THE REASONING CUT, UNIT BY UNIT (prose-sanitizer splitLeadingThought; the developer, 2026-11-12: "please do unit
+ * testing"). Fixtures in the shapes of the prod thoughts (photos of 2026-11-12: hex ids ending a sentence, code with
+ * dots in it, "\r" inside code, numbered lists, quoted rules, "Wait:" and "Let's" lines), not their words.
+ *   [1] headers: what is one, and what is not
+ *   [2] the seam at a chunk boundary, the thought chunked at random (300 times): always cut exactly there
+ *   [3] while it streams: no seam reported before the chunk that brings it; at it, the right one
+ *   [4] joints that are NOT the seam: inside a chunk, inside inline code, a space after the stop
+ *   [5] no chunk seam: the tool block; one chunk carrying both (final); a reply read back later (no bounds)
+ *   [6] answers that start oddly: "I ...", a number, a quote; a thought ending in ":" or ")"
+ * Run: node test/test-thought-cut-unit.mjs
+ */
+import { splitLeadingThought, stripLeadingThought, startsWithThought, maybeThoughtHead } from '../src/agent/prose-sanitizer.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const H = '\u{1FAE7}thought\n';
+// a thought shaped like the prod ones (hashes, `code.with.dots`, \r in code, a list, a quoted rule, Wait:/Let's)
+const THOUGHT = [
+  'That finding was for commit 0a1b2c3, which we already fixed in commit 4d5e6f7 in the previous turn (call_9f8e7d6c5b4a) by writing raw CRLF bytes via node (`fs.writeFileSync`) and committing 4d5e6f7!\n',
+  "Let's verify `git log -n 2` and `git diff --stat 1a2b3c4 HEAD` right now to confirm 4d5e6f7 is HEAD and its check is running (or we just report it). Wait: let's check our instructions:\n",
+  '1. COMMIT IT WITH commit({ message, complexity, files }) ...\n',
+  '2. WHEN THE CHECK FINDS SOMETHING, it comes to you as a message: fix what holds up, and commit() again.\n',
+  'Wait: since 4d5e6f7 was committed BEFORE the check for 0a1b2c3 finished, is 4d5e6f7 being checked? Let\'s check `git status`.\n',
+  'Wait: the file stripped `\\r` when it was committed! We compare `c~1` and `c` with normalized line endings so the diff is 100% surgical!\n',
+  "Let's check Rule:\nA change the developer spells out exactly is already their certified proposal: make it, test it, commit it.\n",
+  "Let's double-check if we need a tool call at all - no! Everything is verified right above!",
+].join('');
+const ANSWER = 'Everything is verified: 4d5e6f7 is HEAD, and the finding on 0a1b2c3 was already fixed there.';
+const FULL = H + THOUGHT + ANSWER;
+
+// a gateway's chunking of the thought, at random - never at a joint with no space (it never splits a sentence that way)
+let seed = 7;
+const rnd = () => { seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff; return seed / 0x7fffffff; };
+function chunkAt(text, from, to, n) {
+  const cuts = new Set();
+  for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+    const c = from + 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * (to - from - 1));
+    if (/[.!?:`)\]"']/.test(text[c - 1]) && /[A-Z]/.test(text[c])) continue;   // (a glued joint: not where a gateway cuts)
+    cuts.add(c);
+  }
+  return [...cuts].sort((a, b) => a - b);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[1] headers');
+ok(['\u{1FAE7}thought\nx', '\u{1FAE7} thought\nx', 'thought\nx', 'Thinking Process:\nx', 'thinking:\nx', '  \u{1FAE7}thought\r\nx'].every(startsWithThought), 'a header: "🫧thought", with a space, bare, "Thinking Process:", "thinking:", CRLF after it');
+ok(!['Thoughtful work.', 'Thinking about it, yes.', 'The thought is good.', 'thought about it\n'].some(startsWithThought), 'not one: "Thoughtful", "Thinking about it", a thought mid-sentence, "thought about it"');
+ok(stripLeadingThought('Just an answer.Here') === 'Just an answer.Here', 'a reply with no header is left exactly as it is, joints and all');
+ok(['\u{1FAE7}', '\u{1FAE7}th', 'Thinking Pro'].every(maybeThoughtHead) && !maybeThoughtHead('The answer'), 'while streaming, only a possible header is held');
+
+console.log('\n[2] the seam at a chunk boundary, the thought chunked at random');
+{
+  const seamAt = H.length + THOUGHT.length;
+  let right = 0, wrong = [];
+  for (let k = 0; k < 300; k++) {
+    const inner = chunkAt(FULL, 0, seamAt, 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * 12));
+    const tail = chunkAt(FULL, seamAt, FULL.length, Math.floor(rnd() * 3)).filter((c) => !(/[.!?:`)\]"']/.test(FULL[c - 1]) && /[A-Z]/.test(FULL[c])));
+    const bounds = [0, ...inner, seamAt, ...tail];
+    const sp = splitLeadingThought(FULL, { bounds });
+    if (sp && sp.seam === 'chunk' && sp.answer === ANSWER && sp.thought === THOUGHT) right++; else if (wrong.length < 2) wrong.push(JSON.stringify(sp && sp.answer).slice(0, 60));
+  }
+  ok(right === 300, `300 random chunkings: cut exactly at the seam every time (${right}/300${wrong.length ? '; e.g. ' + wrong.join(' | ') : ''})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] while it streams');
+{
+  const seamAt = H.length + THOUGHT.length;
+  const bounds = [0, H.length, H.length + 200, H.length + 700, seamAt, seamAt + 30];
+  let early = false, at = null;
+  for (let i = 1; i < bounds.length; i++) {
+    const prefix = FULL.slice(0, bounds[i] + (i === bounds.length - 1 ? 0 : 0));
+    const sp = splitLeadingThought(FULL.slice(0, bounds[i] + 5 > FULL.length ? FULL.length : bounds[i] + 5), { bounds: bounds.slice(0, i + 1) });
+    if (sp && sp.seam === 'chunk') { if (bounds[i] < seamAt) early = true; else if (at == null) at = sp; }
+    void prefix;
+  }
+  ok(!early, 'no seam reported before the chunk that brings the answer');
+  ok(at && at.answer.startsWith('Every'), `at that chunk: the answer begins ("${at && at.answer.slice(0, 20)}")`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] joints that are not the seam');
+{
+  const t = H + 'It calls System.Out and e.g.The config in `Foo.Bar` - then answers now!' + 'Here it is.';
+  const seam = t.indexOf('Here');
+  const b = [0, H.length, seam];
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(t, { bounds: b }).answer === 'Here it is.', 'with chunks: "System.Out", "e.g.The" and "`Foo.Bar`" inside a chunk are passed over');
+  const inCode = H + 'Look at `a.B` here' + '`.Then` more!' + 'Answer.';
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(inCode, { bounds: [0, H.length, H.length + 18, inCode.indexOf('Answer')] }).answer === 'Answer.', 'a chunk boundary INSIDE inline code is not the seam');
+  const spaced = H + 'Done thinking. ' + 'The answer.';
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(spaced, { bounds: [0, H.length, spaced.indexOf('The answer')], final: true }).seam !== 'chunk', 'a boundary after a stop and a SPACE is not the seam (that is how sentences go)');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] no chunk seam');
+{
+  const blk = H + 'Let me look at the file first.\n<remote-workspace><js>return 1;</js></remote-workspace>';
+  const sp = splitLeadingThought(blk, { bounds: [0, H.length], final: true });
+  ok(sp.seam === 'block' && sp.answer.startsWith('<remote-workspace>') && /look at the file/.test(sp.thought), 'reasoning, then the tool block: the block is where the answer begins');
+  const one = H + 'All in one chunk, the gateway sent both!Here it is.';
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(one, { bounds: [0], final: true }).answer === 'Here it is.', 'one chunk with both: at the end, the joint in the text is taken');
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(one, { bounds: [0] }).seam === null, '...but not while it streams (a later chunk may still bring the seam)');
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(H + THOUGHT + ANSWER).answer === ANSWER, 'a reply read back later (no chunks): the joint in the text, outside code');
+  const q = H + 'So that is it."Done" is what it says.';
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(q).answer === '"Done" is what it says.', 'read back later: a quote after the stop opens the answer - it is not cut off it');
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(H + 'He wrote it `x`Next.').seam === null, 'read back later: a backtick with no stop before it is no seam');
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(H + 'I can answer from what I read.`src/orders.js` exports two functions.').answer === '`src/orders.js` exports two functions.', 'read back later: a stop, then a code span opening the answer (live, 2026-11-12)');
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(H + 'It reads it.\nthen sums', { bounds: [0, H.length, H.length + 13], final: true }).seam !== 'chunk', 'a chunk boundary before a small letter (a sentence going on) is no seam');
+  const none = H + 'Only thinking, never an answer';
+  ok(splitLeadingThought(none, { bounds: [0, H.length], final: true }).seam === null && stripLeadingThought(none) === 'Only thinking, never an answer', 'no seam and no block: only the header goes, nothing is guessed away');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] answers and thoughts that end or start oddly');
+for (const [th, ans, what] of [['Right, answer now!', 'I updated the file.', 'an answer starting "I "'], ['So that is it.', '"Done" is what it says.', 'an answer opening with a quote (the quote stays with it)'], ['He wrote "all done."', 'Then it ran.', 'a thought ending in a closing quote after the stop'], ['Here is the plan:', 'First, run the tests.', 'a thought ending in ":"'], ['He said it (that is the call).', 'The call ran.', 'a thought ending in ")."'], ['All good?', 'Yes, all of it passed.', 'a thought ending in "?"'], ['I can answer from what I read.', '`src/orders.js` exports two functions.', 'an answer opening with inline code, a small letter in it (live, 2026-11-12)'], ['That is all.', '**Two** functions, both small.', 'an answer opening in bold'], ['Here goes:', '1. Run the tests.', 'an answer opening with a numbered list'], ['Done.', '- total() sums the items', 'an answer opening with a bullet']]) {
+  const t = H + th + ans;
+  const sp = splitLeadingThought(t, { bounds: [0, H.length, H.length + th.length], final: true });
+  const want = ans;
+  ok(want ? sp.answer === ans : sp.seam !== 'chunk', `${what}: ${want ? 'cut at the boundary' : 'not taken for a seam'} (${JSON.stringify(sp.answer.slice(0, 24))})`);
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const c = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+    const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{3,8}) ---\s*$/.exec(c) || [])[1] || 'zzzz';
+    // (the opening of a reply is held until 300 characters have come - the cannot-run check, turn-stream.mjs CANT_RUN_HOLD -
+    // so the reasoning of [4] is longer than that, as prod's are: it streams from there on)
+    const LONG = ['\u{1FAE7}thought\n', 'Wait: the developer asks where the logger comes from, and the answer depends on how the module graph is wired at start-up, so the start-up code is the place to look first. ', 'Let\'s check the wiring in Foo.Bar first, then the factory that builds the service, then where the factory is called from in the main entry point. ', 'That should settle it. ', 'Right, answer now!', 'The logger is set up in Foo.Bar.'];
+    const chunks = /no seam please/.test(c) ? ['\u{1FAE7}thought\n', 'just thinking, ', 'no answer split off'] : /where is it set up/.test(c) ? LONG : CHUNKS;
+    for (const x of chunks) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: x } }] }; await sleep(DELAY); }
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: '\n/--- you ' + tag + ' ---' } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-tf-proj-'));

+  await sleep(500);

+  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { const s = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json(); if (s.status !== 'running' && i > 5) break; await sleep(100); }
