+/**
+ * WHAT THE WORK SAYS OF ITSELF (the developer, 2026-11-12): checked, choices, different - fields of commit() and done(),
+ * all required ("none" when one does not apply), shown under the message the developer sees, and handed to the check
+ * as claims to test.
+ *   [1] workReportOf: all three required, named in the refusal, with what each is
+ *   [2] workReportLines: the labelled lines the page draws; a "none" is not added
+ *   [3] reviewTask: the three as claims, each with what to test
+ *   [4] commit() and done() refuse without them, before anything is committed or checked
+ * Run: node test/test-work-report.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
+
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-workrep-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+const { workReportOf, workReportLines, execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+const { reviewTask } = await import('../src/agent/review-loop.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] all three required');
+  let err = '';
+  try { workReportOf({ checked: 'ran it' }, 'commit'); } catch (e) { err = e.message; }
+  ok(/commit\(\): different, choices are required/.test(err) && /Write "none" when one does not apply/.test(err) && /a check that CAN FAIL/.test(err), `the missing ones named, with what each is (${err.slice(0, 90)})`);
+  const r = workReportOf({ checked: 'ran the 14 tests', choices: ['dates before 1970 invalid', 'UTC'], different: 'none' }, 'done');
+  ok(r.checked === 'ran the 14 tests' && r.choices === 'dates before 1970 invalid; UTC' && r.different === 'none', 'given: kept, a list joined');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the lines under the message');
+  const l = workReportLines(r);
+  ok(l === 'Choices: dates before 1970 invalid; UTC\nChecked: ran the 14 tests', `labelled as the page reads them, the "none" not added (${JSON.stringify(l)})`);
+  ok(workReportLines({ checked: 'None.', choices: 'none', different: 'n/a' }) === '', 'all "none": nothing added');
+  ok(workReportLines({ checked: 'one\nand two' }) === 'Checked: one and two', 'one line each');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the check gets them as claims');
+  const t = reviewTask({ kind: 'change', brief: 'b', report: r });
+  ok(/WHAT THE WORK SAYS OF ITSELF/.test(t) && /Checked: ran the 14 tests/.test(t) && /could it have FAILED/.test(t), 'Checked: did it run, could it fail');
+  ok(/Different from what you asked: none/.test(t) && /NOT said here/.test(t), 'Different: what was done otherwise and not said');
+  ok(/Choices: dates before 1970/.test(t) && /the other reasonable reading change the result/.test(t), 'Choices: would the other reading change the result');
+  ok(!/WHAT THE WORK SAYS OF ITSELF/.test(reviewTask({ kind: 'change', brief: 'b' })), 'no report: no section');
+
+  console.log('\n[4] commit() and done() refuse without them');
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-workrep-proj-'));
+  execFileSync('git', ['init', '-q'], { cwd: proj });
+  execFileSync('git', ['-c', 'user.email=t@t', '-c', 'user.name=t', 'commit', '-q', '--allow-empty', '-m', 'first'], { cwd: proj });
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'a.txt'), 'x\n');
+  const started = [];
+  const ctx = { cwd: proj, projectDir: proj, commitReview: { start: async (x) => { started.push(x); return { agentId: 'c1', round: 1 }; } } };
+  const run = async (code) => extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, ctx));
+  const c1 = await run('return await commit({ message: "m", complexity: 1, review: "r", certify: "c", files: ["a.txt"] });');
+  const head = execFileSync('git', ['log', '--oneline'], { cwd: proj }).toString().trim().split('\n').length;
+  ok(/different, choices, checked are required/.test(c1) && head === 1 && !started.length, `commit() without them: refused, nothing committed, no check (${head} commit)`);
+  const c2 = await run('return await commit({ message: "m", complexity: 1, review: "r", certify: "c", files: ["a.txt"], checked: "cat a.txt shows x", choices: "none", different: "none" });');
+  ok(/committed/.test(c2) && started.length === 1 && started[0].report && started[0].report.checked === 'cat a.txt shows x', 'with them: committed, and the check is handed them');
+  const d1 = await run('return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "the cause", brief: "b" });');
+  ok(/different, choices, checked are required/.test(d1) && started.length === 1, 'done() without them: refused, no check');
+  const d2 = await run('return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "the cause", brief: "b", checked: "reproduced it", choices: "none", different: "none" });');
+  ok(/Being checked/.test(d2) && started.length === 2 && started[1].report.checked === 'reproduced it', 'with them: checked');
+  await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+} finally {
+  await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
